Also जातकदेश

त्रिंशद्वयो येऽधिकाक्षा अपि शरकृतितो राशयो ये तदुना:
श्रेष्ठा मध्याश्च कथा: क्रमशः इति मता गृह्यतां श्रेष्ठराशिः ।
सर्वांसिष्टक्रियासु त्यजतु न गमनाद्येषु कार्येषु कष्टान्
सम्बन्धे संपदापत्सति युवतिनृणां श्रेष्ठष्टर्क्षकानां ॥

ये तुङ्गराशिखसुहृद्‌गृहास्था ये कोणकेन्द्रोपचयस्थिताश्च ।
ये सौम्यवर्गादिबलोपयातास्ते नाशदा लाघवबिन्दुकाश्वेत् ॥ ५४ ॥

*Sloka* 54. Planets occupying, it may be, their exaltation, their own, a friend’s house, a Trikona, a Kendra, or an <term>उपचय</term> (Upachaya) place or possessed of such strength as is derived from being in benefic vargas, may do evil if associated with insufficient benefic dots (in the <term>सर्वाष्टकवर्ग</term>-Sarvashtakavarga).

ये चावसानरिपुरन्ध्रतुरङ्गभागा:
ये नीचपापरिपुखेचरवर्गयुक्ता: ।
ये मान्दिराशिपतिना सह बाधकाश्च
ते सर्वमुख्यफलदास्त्वधिबिन्दुकाश्वेत् ॥ ५५ ॥

*Sloka* 55. Planets which as occupying portions of the 12th, the 6th, the 8th or the 7th <term>bhavas</term> are considered malefic ; secondly, those that are in the vargas of their depression signs or of malefic or inimical planets ; 3rdly and lastly, those that conspire in conjunction with the lord of the sign occupied by <term>मान्दि</term> (Mandhi) to work evil—these three classes of planets, if only associated with a higher number of benefic dots, produce all important beneficial results to the person concerned.

मानस्थिताल्लाभगृहे बहुत्वे लाभाल्लघुत्वे यदि रिःफराशौ ।
रिःफोपयातादधिके विलग्ने जातः सुखी वित्तयशोबलाढ्यः ॥ ५६ ॥

--- Page 79 ---
<lang>Sanskrit, English</lang>
<page-num>702</page-num>
<header>जातकपारिजाते Adh. X.</header>

*Sloka 60.* If a person have his birth under a yoga betokening great prosperity, he will, upon the expiry of a number of years indicated by the <term>सर्वाष्टकवर्ग</term> (<term>Sarvashtaka Varga</term>) figures in the <term>Lagna</term>, be in a fair way to get a royal vehicle (such as a palanquin), riches, sons and knowledge surpassingly great.

रिःफाधीशो मन्दगेहोदयस्थे होरारन्ध्रस्वामिनो दुर्बलौ च ।
लग्ने यावद्विन्दुसंख्यास्तदीया जातस्यायुर्वत्सराः सम्भवन्ति ॥ ६१ ॥

*Sloka 61.* When the lord of the 12th <term>bhava</term> occupies the 1st represented by a <term>Rasi</term> belonging to Saturn, and when the lords of the <term>Lagna</term> and the 8th <term>bhava</term> are weak, the person born will live as many years as there are benefic dots in the <term>Lagna</term> in the <term>सर्वाष्टकवर्ग</term> (<term>Sarvashtaka Varga</term>).

यानाधीशो लग्ने चाङ्गस्थे लग्नाधीशो तद्ग्रहोपेतराश्योः ।
त्रिंशत्त्रयसंख्याविन्दवः स्युर्यश्चेत् जाता राजश्रीनिदाना नरेन्द्राः ६२

*Sloka 62.* When the lord of the 4th <term>bhava</term> is in the <term>Lagna</term> and the lord thereof is in the 4th, and when the number of benefic dots in each of the <term>Rasis</term> occupied by these planets is 33, the persons born will become lords of men and centres of kingly glory.

होरान्धुप्राप्तिभावत्रयेषु त्रिंशन्मानाधिक्यविद्वृपगेषु ।
जातस्तेजःश्रीबहुत्वं च राज्यं चत्वारिंशद्वत्सरादूर्ध्वमेति ॥ ६३ ॥

*Sloka 63.* When the 1st, 4th and the 11th <term>bhavas</term> are each associated with benefic dots exceeding 30, the person born will, after his 40th year, attain to power and prosperity in abundance culminating in kingship.

->यत्पञ्चविंशतिमुखास्त्रि दशान्तसंख्या<-
->बन्धुस्थिता नवमराशिकबिन्दवश्च ।<-

<vocab>Jatakaparijata, Sloka, yoga, Sarvashtaka Varga, Lagna, bhava, Rasi, Saturn</vocab>

<sig>Sl. 64-67</sig>

->यद्यष्टकेन सह विंशतिवत्सराणा-<-
->मन्ते परे शरदि वा नरवाहनाढ्यः ॥ ६४ ॥<-

*Sloka 64.* If the number of benefic dots in the 4th and in the 9th Rasi from the Lagna lie between 25 and 30, the person born will be rich as Kubera at the end of 28 years or in some year subsequent thereto.

->देवाचार्ये वाहनस्थे स्वतुङ्गे चत्वारिंशद्विन्दुसंख्यासमेते ।<-
->मेषागारे लग्ने वासरेशो जातो राजा लक्षसंख्याश्वनाथः ॥ ६५ ॥<-

*Sloka 65.* When Jupiter occupying the 4th bhava in exaltation is associated with 40 benefic dots and the Sun is in Mesha identical with the Lagna, the person born will become a king and be in command of a 100,000 horse.

->चत्वारिंशद्विन्दुयुक्ते विलग्ने चापे जीवे भार्गवे मीनराशौ ।<-
->स्वोच्चे भौमे कुम्भगे भानुपुत्रे जातः सर्वश्रीधरः सार्वभौमः ॥ ६६ ॥<-

*Sloka 66.* With 40 benefic dots in the Lagna, with Jupiter in Dhanus, with Venus in Meena, with Mars in exaltation and with Saturn in Kumbha, the person born should become an emperor blessed with every kind of good fortune.

->क्रियादिराशित्रितयोपयाता भवन्ति पूर्वादिचतुर्दिशश्च ।<-
->फलाधिकं यद्दिशि तत्प्रदेशे धनादिवृद्धिं समुपैति जातः ॥ ६७ ॥<-

*Sloka 67.* The four quarters beginning with the East have been assigned to the four triads of Rasis whereof the first are Mesha, Vrishabha, Mithuna and Kataka (*Vide Adhyaya I sl. 13*). With reference to a horoscope that may be under examination, ascertain the quarter in which there is an abundance of benefic dots ;

**Sloka 56.** If in the **सर्वाष्टकवर्ग** (Sarvashtaka Varga) the number of benefic dots in the 11th bhava be greater than that in the 10th, but the figure in the 12th lower than that in the 11th and if the figure in the Lagna exceed the figure in the 12th bhava, the person born will be happy with abundance of wealth, fame and strength.

<margin>cf. फलदीपा</margin>
->मध्यात्फलाधिकं लाभे लाभात्क्षीणतरे व्यये ।<-
->यस्य व्ययाधिकं लग्ने भोगवानर्थवान् भवेत् ॥<-

->खण्डत्रयं झषकरकटकीटकाद्यं<-
->तच्चचतुष्टयगृहोपगबिन्दुयुक्तम् ।<-
->आद्यञ्च मध्यमवसानमिति प्रयुक्तं<-
->केचिद्व्ययादिकमिह प्रवदन्ति लोके ॥ ५७ ॥<-

**Sloka 57.** Three divisions made of the 12 signs, the first commencing with Meena, the second with Karkataka and the third with Vrischika and exhibiting each its own benefic dots in its respective 4 houses are made to indicate the first, the middle and the concluding portion of the life under examination. Some opine that the three grand divisions are made from the **व्ययभाव** (Vyayabhava) to begin with.

<margin>cf. जातक देश</margin>
->मीनेन्द्राळयवृश्चिकप्रभृतिकं खण्डत्रयं कल्पये-<-
->दाद्येऽक्षाधिकतादिमे तु वयसस्त्र्यंशे विदध्यात्सुखम् ।<-
->मध्ये मध्यवयस्यपान्तिमवयस्त्र्यंशेऽन्त्यखण्डे हि सा<-
->हीनाक्षस्तु वयस्त्रिभाग इह योत्र व्याविदुःखोद्भवः ॥<-

->विन्दुं त्यक्त्वा रिःफरन्ध्रोपयातं शिष्टं खण्डं केचिदिच्छन्ति सन्तः ।<-
->तुल्यस्वल्पाधिक्यविन्दुक्रमेण मिश्रं दुःखं सम्पदः स्युर्नराणाम् ५८ .<-

<vocab>Sarvashtaka Varga, Bhava, Lagna, Meena, Karkataka, Vrischika, Vyayabhava, Jatakaparijata, Phaladeepika, Jataka Desha, झष, करकट, कीटक, रिःफ, रन्ध्र, इन्द्रालय</vocab>

--- Page 82 ---
<lang>English, Sanskrit</lang>
<page-num>701</page-num>
<header>Sl. 58-60 दशमोऽध्यायः</header>

*Sloka 58.* Some recommend the omission of the benefic dots in the 12th as well as in the 8th <term>bhava</term> from their several divisions; the aggregate benefic dots in any one division shou'd be compared with the same in the other two. If the figures in the three divisions be equal, the life of the person concerned would be a mixed one with its joys and sorrows evenly distributed; if unequal, the lower figure implies diminution of comfort, *i.e.* misery; the higher one betokens increased comforts, *i.e.* prosperity.

->सौम्याक्रान्तं यदि सुखकरं मिश्रदं मिश्रयोगे<-
->खण्डं पापग्रहचरसहितं क्लेशयोगाकरं स्यात् ।<-
->बिन्दुस्वल्पो यदि गदमनस्तापवान् पापवादी<-
->बिन्द्वाधिक्ये वयसि विपुलश्रीसमेतः प्रजातः ॥ ५९ ॥<-

*Sloka 59.* If benefic planets occupy the signs of any division, the portion of life corresponding to this खण्ड (<term>khanda</term>) will be happy. If the signs of a खण्ड (<term>khanda</term>) have both malefic and benefic planets in them, the effect on the portion of the life typified by the खण्ड (<term>khanda</term>) will be mixed; if any division contains malefic planets exclusively, the period of life represented will abound in accessions of affliction. If the number of benefic dots in any division be small, the person born will suffer from disease and mental pain and will be speaking evil things. If there be a preponderance of benefic dots in a खण्ड (<term>Khanda</term>), the person concerned will enjoy extensive prosperity in the period of life thus distinguished.

->यावद्बिन्दुर्दुलभगस्तावदीयसंख्यातीते वत्सरे राजयानम् ।<-
->वित्तं पुत्रं चातिविद्यामुपैति जातः सम्पद्योगशाली नरश्चेत् ॥ ६० ॥<-

<vocab>Sloka, bhava, khanda, benefic dots, malefic, prosperity, Jyotisha, Dashamoadhyayah</vocab>

--- Page 83 ---
<page-num>704</page-num>
<header>जातकपारिजाते Adh. X.</header>
<lang>Sanskrit, English</lang>

it is there that the owner of the horoscope will get an accession to his wealth, etc.

---

लग्नादिसन्यन्तगतं तुरङ्गैः संगुण्यताराहृतलब्धयाते ।
रव्यादि पापे यदि कोणगे वा रोगादिपीडा विपुला नराणाम् ॥

*Sloka 68.* Find the aggregate of benefic dots from the <term>Lagna</term> to Saturn (both inclusive) Multiply it by 7 and divide the product by 27. When, in the <term>asterism</term> indicated by the remainder (reckoned from <term>Aswini</term>) or its <term>Trikona</term>, the Sun and other malignant planets pass, the person concerned will suffer from diseases and other ills on a large scale.

मन्दादिलग्नान्तफलं च तद्वल्लग्नान्तमारादुदयात् कुजान्तम् ।
शुभैक्यसंख्यागततारकायां शुभग्रहे सौख्यफलं वदन्ति ॥ ६९ ॥

*Sloka 69.* Deal in the above way with the aggregate of benefic dots counted from Saturn to <term>Lagna</term>, and with those from Mars to <term>Lagna</term>, as well as from <term>Lagna</term> to Mars, severally. Find the aggregate of benefic dots in the <term>Rasis</term> occupied by benefic planets and the star indicated thereby. When a benefic planet passes through this <term>asterism</term>, good things, they say, may be expected.

### NOTES.

cf. <term>फलदीपिका</term>
> लग्नात्प्रभृति मन्दान्तमेकीकृत्य फलानि वै ।
> सप्तभिर्गुणयेत्पश्चादृक्षविंशहृतात्फलम् ॥
> तत्समागत वर्षे दुःखं वा रोगमाप्नुयात् ।
> एवं मन्दादिलग्नान्तं भौमराहोस्तथा फलम् ॥

Also <term>जातकआदेश</term>
> लग्नादारभ्य सूर्यात्मजगतभवनाक्षान्तमेकत्र युक्त्या
> सुघ्ने तस्मिन् सुखाप्ते गतवति फलतुल्याब्दके रोगशोकः ।

<header>Sl. 70 दशमोऽध्यायः</header>

->मन्दादालग्नमेवं क्षितिसुतगतयाञ्चालिलग्नं विलग्ना-<-
->द्रामौमंत्वेतिकृत्वा विधिमशुभमतिवादिशेच्चोदिताब्दे ॥<-

The following additional information based on the <term>सर्वाष्टकवर्ग</term> (**Sarvashtakavarga**) figures are extracted from **जातकदेश**.

->लग्नाभ्यात्मजकामधर्मगगनस्याक्षाणि संयोजये-<-
->दन्तर्भाग् इहायमत्र फलबाहुल्ये मनस्तुष्टता ।<-
->विद्याज्ञानसुकर्मदाननिरतिध्यान्यस्थिताक्षान्वयो<-
->भागोमन्यत्र फलाधिके सति मनःपीडा च दंभादिकम् ॥<-
->लग्नाध्यक्षचतुस्त्रिकोणभवनं बन्ध्वाह्वयं सेवकै<-
->तद्वत्पोषकघातकाह्वयममीष्वक्षाणि संयोजयेत् ।<-
->आधिक्यं खलु पोषकस्य यदि चेद्धन्तुर्धनी स्यादतः<-
->दारिद्र्यं यदि पोषकादधिकता स्याद्घातकस्य ध्रुवम् ॥<-
->केन्द्रस्थाक्षं पणपरगतमापोक्लिमगतं च युक्त्वापि ।<-
->तेषामल्पिकाल्पात्प्रथमदि वयःशुभाशुभं ज्ञेयम् ॥<-
->यो राशिः फणिनाश्रितोऽथगकलैः संख्यासमे वत्सरे<-
->नृणां पन्नगदशनं गरलभुक्तिर्वाहिपेऽनिष्टगे ।<-
->वर्गे भौमगतालयस्थितफलैस्तुल्ये तु शस्त्रक्षति-<-
->र्मन्दाक्रान्तिफलैः समानवयसि स्यु रोगशोकादयः ॥<-

**एकाधिपत्यं सहकोणभावैः संशोष्य सन्त्यज्य दिनेशमानैः ।**
**यद्यर्कसंख्या न हरेदशेषं मेपादि सर्वाष्टकशोधितं स्यात् ॥ ७० ॥**

*Sloka 70.* Make the <term>Trikona</term> and <term>Ekadhipatya</term> reductions in regard to the <term>सर्वाष्टकवर्ग</term> (**Sarvashtakavarga**) figures. Out of the figures left after the two reductions in the several <term>Rasis</term>, go on subtracting 12 as often as possible. If the reduced figure happens to be 12 itself, it is not to be taken away. The figures contained in <term>Mesha</term> and other <term>Rasis</term> are to be thus treated.

<sig>89</sig>

<vocab>Sarvashtakavarga, Jatakadesha, Trikona, Ekadhipatya, Rasi, Mesha, astrology, reduction, shloka</vocab>

<header>जातकपारिजाते Adh. XI</header>

---

->अष्टवर्गं समुद्धृत्य ग्रहाणां राशिमण्डले ।<-
->प्राग्वत्त्रिकोणं संशोध्य पश्चादेकाधिपत्यताम् ॥<-
->एकस्मिन् मण्डलाधिक्यं शोधयेच्चक्रमण्डलम् ।<-
->द्वादशैव तु गृह्णीयादेवं सर्वेषु राशिषु ॥<-
->पूर्वोक्तगुणकारैस्तु वर्धयेच्च पृथक् पृथक् ।<-
->एकीकृत्य ततः सर्वं सप्तभिर्गुणयेत्पुनः ॥<-
->सप्तविंशतिहृल्लब्धं आयुःपिण्डः प्रशस्यते ।<-
->द्वादशादिगुणाल्लब्धं मासादिवटिकास्ततः ॥<-
->शतादूर्ध्वं तु तत्पिण्डं मण्डलं शोधयेच्च तम् ।<-
->शतमेव तु गृह्णीयादीर्वायुर्योगसंभवी ॥<-
->तद्दिधं सकलं कृत्वा खराङ्गेन विवर्धितम् ।<-
->मातङ्गहृल्लब्धायुर्मातङ्गाब्दं प्रशस्यते ॥<-

->शोध्यं राशिचरगुणकैः सङ्गुणय्यैतदैक्यं<-
->हृत्वा शैलैरुडुगणहृतं लब्धमब्दादिकं स्यात् ।<-
->मानाधिक्ये विशतमशते तारकायुर्वराङ्गैः<-
->हत्वा भक्तं दिवसनिचयैर्मातुलैः शुद्धमायुः ॥ ७१ ॥<-

*Sloka 71.* Multiply the net figures in the (सर्वाष्टकवर्ग) <term>Sarvashtakavarga</term> (after the reductions mentioned in the previous sloka have been made) by the zodiacal and planetary factors and make an aggregate of the products. Multiply this by 7 and divide the product by 27. The quotient consisting of years, months, days, etc., if in excess of the standard <term>Ayus</term>, i.e. 100 years, should be diminished by 100 years; if short of 100 is to be kept unaltered. This will represent the <term>नक्षत्रायुस्</term> (<term>Nakshatra Ayus</term>) which when multiplied by 324 and divided by 365 will give the period of life correctly in solar years.

<vocab>Jataka Parijata, Sarvashtakavarga, Nakshatra Ayus, Ayus, Sloka, zodiacal factors, planetary factors</vocab>

<header>SI. 72-73 दशमोऽध्यायः 707</header>

->स्वल्पमध्यबहुमानवत्सरा मण्डलोनयुतकर्मकल्पिताः ।<-
->तुल्यकालमुपयान्ति सर्वतः सद्गुणोदयकटाक्षवीक्षणात् ॥ ७२ ॥<-

> **Sloka 72.** The years making up the life whether short, middle or long of a person (obtained by the process above mentioned) will square with the period otherwise obtained, by the diminution or addition as the case may be of a multiple of 27 ascertained upon an examination of the aspect, if any, upon the <term>Lagna</term>, by benefic planets.

->श्रीवैद्यनाथकृतजातकपारिजाते<-
->पाराशरादिफलसारसोपयाते ।<-
->प्रस्तारभिन्नसमुदायकबिन्दुशीलः<-
->संकीर्तितस्तु सकलद्युचरप्रसादात् ॥ ७३ ॥<-

->**॥ इति दशमोऽध्यायः ॥**<-

> **Sloka 73.** In the work of <term>Jatakaparijata</term> compiled by <term>Vaidyanatha</term> and containing the essence of treatises on planetary effects such as <term>Parasara's</term>, the chapter on benefic dots, appearing in the <term>Ashtakavargas</term> designated <term>प्रस्तार</term> (Prasthara), <term>भिन्न</term> (Bhinna) and <term>समुदाय</term> (Samudaya) has been treated by the grace of the entire body of planets.

द्वादशांकहताच्छेषं यत्तद्राशिगते रवौ ।
मृत्युं तत्र विजानीयात्तत्त्रिकोणमथापि वा ॥
एवमेव विजानीयात्पितृमातृगृहादिषु ।
लग्नादष्टमपाद्वापि सर्वमेतद्विचारयेत् ॥
अर्कस्फुटकला: स्थाप्या: राहो: स्फुटकलाहता: ।
चक्रकलाहताल्लब्धं योजयेद्भास्करे स्फुटे ॥
तादृशे भास्करे यस्मिन् तस्मिन्मासे मृतिं वदेत् ।
तत्त्रिकोणगते वापि निधनं तत्र निर्दिशेत् ॥
अष्टमाधिपतिर्नीचे चन्द्रे षष्ठाष्टमेपि वा ।
लग्नाष्टमरिपुस्थस्स तन्मासे मरणं वदेत् ॥

## अथ निधनचन्द्रज्ञानम् ।

अष्टमेशे त्रिकोणस्थे चन्द्रे च निधनं वदेत् ।
जन्मलग्नांशकाच्चन्द्रनवांशादथवापि वा ॥
राहौ चतु:षष्ठिमिते निधनं च विनिर्दिशेत् ।
जन्मलग्नाष्टमे राशौ जन्मलग्नोदयेपि वा ॥
लग्ननीचोदये वापि तेषां शुद्धोदये मृति: ॥

->End of the 10th Adhyaya.<-

# जातकपारिजाते एकादशोऽध्यायः

->॥ अथ भावफलानि ॥<-

->Adhyaya XI.<-

->THE EFFECTS OF THE 1ST AND THE 2ND BHAVAS.<-

> होरालंकारमुख्यप्रथमवसुमफलादीनि सर्वाणि पुंसां
> तत्तद्भावोद्भवानि द्युचरबलवशाद्यानि तानि प्रवक्ष्ये ।
> ये ये भावाः सितज्ञामरगुरुपतिभिः संयुता वीक्षिता वा
> नान्यैर्दृष्टा न युक्ता यदि शुभफलदा मूर्तिमात्रादिकेषु ॥१॥

**Sloka 1.** I am to treat of the good and other effects which are the chief source of adornment to the science of astrology just as they arise in the several bhavas appertaining to men owing to the preponderating influence of planets. Among the Lagna and other bhavas, such as are occupied or aspected by **Venus**, **Mercury**, **Jupiter** or the <term>भावपति</term> (**Bhavapathi**—the lord of the bhava concerned), but unaspected by or unassociated with the rest, produce happy results.

<margin>cf. उत्तरकालामृत</margin>
> यो यो भावः स्वामिदृष्टो युतो वा सौम्यैर्वा स्यात्तस्य तस्याभिवृद्धिः ।
> पापैरेवं तस्य भावस्य हानिर्निर्देष्टव्या प्रश्नतो मन्मतो वा ॥

> तन्वादिभावेषु शुभोदयेषु तद्भावनाथोपगवेक्षितेषु ।
> तदुक्तभावस्वसमृद्धिरुक्ता न पापखेटेक्षितसंयुतेषु ॥ २ ॥

<vocab>Jatakaparijata, Adhyaya, Bhavaphala, Sloka, Lagna, Bhavapathi, Uttarakalamrita, astrology, planets</vocab>

--- Page 89 ---
<lang>en</lang>
<page-num>710</page-num>
<header>जातकपारिजाते Adh. XI.</header>

### Sloka 2.
When the <term>Lagna</term> and some of the other <term>bhavas</term> have <term>benefic</term> planets appearing in them or are occupied or aspected by their lords, a wealth of the said <term>bhavas</term> may be declared to exist provided they are unaspected or unoccupied by <term>malefic</term> planets.

---

->नीचस्थो रिपुराशिस्थः खेटो भावविनाशकः ।<-
->मूलस्वतुङ्गमित्रस्थो भाववृद्धिकरो भवेत् ॥ ३ ॥<-

### Sloka 3.
A planet in its depression or inimical sign destroys the <term>bhava</term> that it owns. But in its <term>मूलत्रिकोण</term> (<term>Moolatrikona</term>), exaltation or friendly sign it advances the same.

<margin>cf. उत्तरपाराशर</margin>
> यद्भावेशोऽरिनीचस्थो मूढो वा तन्न पश्यति ।
> तद्भावतत्त्वमालस्यं वैरित्वं वा विनिर्दिशेत् ॥
> भावेशः कारकोऽपि स्वगृहगुणगतौ तुङ्गमित्रौ यदा ता-
> वन्योन्यस्थौ शुभाभ्यामित इति युतौ वीक्षितौ सौम्यदृष्टौ ।
> एतेषां भावपुष्टिर्भवति यदि हीनौ दुःस्थलस्थौ युतौ वा
> दृष्टौ पापापचरैर्न भवति रिपुनीचास्तयुक्तौ च हानिः ॥
> भावं पश्यति वा स्थितोत्र यदि वा यः कोऽपि भावाधिपः
> तुङ्गान्योन्यसुहृन्निकेतनगतो दृष्टः शुभैर्मध्यगः ।
> भावस्यास्ति फलं न चास्ति यदि चेद्भावाधिपो दुःस्थितो
> नीचास्तारिगृहं गतो यदि भवेत्पापैर्युतो वीक्षितः ॥

---

->यद्भावनाथो रिपुरिःफरन्ध्रे दुःस्थानपो यद्भावस्थितो वा ।<-
->तद्भावनाशं कथयन्ति तज्ज्ञाः शुभेक्षितश्चेत्फलमन्यथा स्यात् ॥ ४ ॥<-

### Sloka 4.
When a <term>bhava</term> has its lord in the 6th, the 8th or the 12th, or is occupied by the lord of one of these three, it suffers annihilation, say those that know.

<vocab>Lagna, bhava, Sloka, Moolatrikona, depression, inimical sign, exaltation, Uttara Parasara, benefic, malefic, Jataka Parijata</vocab>

--- Page 90 ---
<lang>Sanskrit, English</lang>
<page-num>711</page-num>
<header>Sl. 5-6 एकादशोऽध्यायः 711</header>

the properties of a bhava If such a bhava be aspected by a benefic planet, the effect will be different.

यद्भावप: केन्द्रगतो विलग्नात् त्रिकोणगो वा यदि सौम्यदृष्ट: ।
तुङ्गादिवर्गोपगतो बलाढ्यस्तद्भावपुष्टि फलमाहुराया: ॥ ५ ॥

**Sloka 5.** If the lord of a bhava occupying a Kendra or Trikona position from the Lagna possess abundant strength being either aspected by benefic planets or in its exaltation and other Vargas, that bhava, say the wise astrologers, is in a flourishing condition.

<insert>उत्तरपाराशरे</insert>

> भावेश: कारकोऽपि स्वगृहगुणगतौ तुङ्गगौ वा मिथस्थौ
> मित्रे मध्यं शुभानां यदि भवति मिथस्थानगौ भावपुष्टि: ।
> अन्योन्यं शत्रुभूतौ यदपि च भवतो नीचभावं गतौ वा
> मूढौ वा पापयुक्तौ तु शुभनिध्नगौ भावनाशं गणन्ति ॥

तत्तद्भावत्रिकोणे सुखमदनगृहे वाऽऽस्पदे सौम्ययुक्ते
पापानां दृष्टिहीने भवनपतिसहिते पापखेटैरयुक्ते ।
भावानां पुष्टिमाहु: सकलशुभकरीमन्यथा चेत्प्रणाशं
मिश्रं मिश्रग्रहेन्द्रैरखिलमपि तथा मूर्तिभावादिकानाम् ॥ ६ ॥

**Sloka 6.** Astrologers pronounce the strength of a bhava such as the Lagna to be ample and thoroughly beneficent when a Trikona, the 4th, the 7th or the 10th place therefrom is occupied by an auspicious planet or <term>भावप</term> (bhavapa—the lord of the bhava) and is unoccupied or unaspected by malefic planets. A bhava suffers decay when the positions referred to above are differently occupied and aspected The effect of a bhava will be mixed when the positions noted above are occupied or aspected by benefic and malefic planets promiscuously.

<vocab>bhava, Lagna, Kendra, Trikona, Sloka, bhavapa, Uttaraparashara, varga</vocab>

--- Page 91 ---
<lang>Sanskrit</lang>
<page-num>712</page-num>
<header>जातकपारिजाते Adh.-XI.</header>

<warning>Image is slightly grainy with some ink bleed-through from the reverse side of the page.</warning>

->नाशस्थानगतो दिवाकरकरैश्छन्नस्तु यद्भावपो<-
->नीचारातिगतोऽथवा यदि शुभैः खेटैरयुक्तेक्षितः ।<-
->तद्भावस्य विनाशनं मुनिगणाः शंसन्ति खेटैर्युतो<-
->यद्यत्रापि फलप्रदो नहि तथा मूर्त्यादिभानां क्रमात् ॥ ७ ॥<-

*Sloka* 7. Of the Lagna and other bhavas examined in succession, whichever bhava has its lord occupying the 8th place (from it) or obscured by the solar rays or in depression or in an inimical house, while no benefic planets aspect or are associated with it, the astrologers declare the total destruction of such a bhava. The lord of the bhava concerned even in the event of being associated with other planets is incapable of producing any good effect.

->दुःस्थाने वाऽरिगे मूढे दुर्बले भावनायके ।<-
->भावस्य संपदं कर्तुं न शक्ता भावमाश्रिताः ॥ ८ ॥<-

*Sloka* 8. Planets occupying a bhava cannot advance it when its lord is in a <term>दुःस्थान</term> (Duḥsthāna—6th, 8th or 12th place from it) or an inimical house or in a state of eclipse or impotence.

->दुःस्थितो वाऽपि यदा नभोगः पापारिनीचांशकसंयुतो यः ।<-
->स्वतुङ्गमित्रंशकराशियुक्तः शुभेक्षितो वा यदि शोभनः स्यात् ॥ ९ ॥<-

*Sloka* 9. A planet may even be badly placed or occupy a <term>नवांश</term> (Navamsa) owned by a malefic, inimical or its own depression sign ; it will nevertheless prove beneficial when the Navamsa or Rasi occupied by it is its own, or that of its exaltation sign or of a friendly planet no less than when benefic planets aspect it.

->भावेशाक्रान्तराशीशे दुःस्थे भावस्य दुर्बलम् ।<-
->स्वोच्चमित्रस्वराशस्थे भावपुष्टिं वदेद्बुधः ॥ १० ॥<-

<vocab>जातकपारिजात (Jataka Parijata), Lagna, Bhava, दुःस्थान (Duḥsthāna), नवांश (Navamsa), Rasi, astrologers</vocab>

--- Page 92 ---
<lang>san</lang>
<page-num>713</page-num>
<header>Sl. 10-12 एकादशोऽध्यायः 713</header>
<warning>The image is a bit grainy, and some characters have small printing artifacts or marks above them.</warning>

*Sloka 10.* When the planet owning the sign occupied by the lord of a bhava is badly placed, the bhava becomes weak. But when the planet referred to above is in its exaltation, in a friendly sign or in its own house, the astrologer may pronounce the bhava to be flourishing.

यद्भावलाभधनविक्रमराशियांता यद्भावनाथसुहृदश्च तदुच्चनाथः ।
तद्भावपुष्टिविमलमम्बरचारिणस्ते कुर्वन्ति मूढरिपुनीचविवर्जिताश्चेत् ॥

*Sloka 11.* When the lord of a bhava, the friends of the <term>भावप</term> (bhavapa) and the lord of the exaltation sign of the <term>भावप</term> (bhavapa) occupy the 11th, the 2nd and the 3rd places in respect to the bhava, those planets advance the strength of the bhava, provided they be not eclipsed or in an unfriendly sign or in depression.

->उत्तरपाराशर<-
केन्द्रे कोणत्रिलाभे धनपतिभवने कारका भावनाथा
भावप्राप्तिर्यदा ते व्ययनिधनरिपुस्थास्तदीशा न दुःस्थाः ।
भावप्राप्तिस्ततोऽपि व्ययरिपुनिधनेपूच्चमित्रस्वभावा-
दन्योऽन्यस्यास्तदीशाः खलु भवति महच्छ्रेय एवान्यथा स्यात् ॥

भावांशतुल्यः खलु वर्तमानभावोद्भवं पूर्णफलं विधत्ते ।
भावोनके चाप्यधिके च खेटे त्रैराशिकेनात्र फलं विचार्यम् ॥ १२ ॥

*Sloka 12.* A planet produces the full effect of the bhava in which it is when its distance from the bhavasandhi is equal to that of the <term>भावांश</term> (bhavamsa = semi-bhava). When the planet has a less or greater distance from a bhava-sandhi than a bhavamsa, its effect must be ascertained by a *rule-of-three* process.

->NOTES.<-

This sloka is taken from <term>श्रीपतिपद्धति</term> (Sripatipaddhati).

<sig>1</sig>

<vocab>bhava, bhavapa, bhavamsa, bhavasandhi, Uttara Parashara, Sripatipaddhati, rule-of-three, एकादशोऽध्यायः</vocab>

## ॥ अथ लग्नभावफलम् ॥

शरीरवर्णाकृतिलक्षणानि यशोगुणस्थानसुखासुखानि ।
प्रवासतेजोबलदुर्बलानि फलानि लग्नस्य वदन्ति सन्तः ॥ १३ ॥

*Sloka 13.* The body, its hue, its form, its characteristics, fame, qualities, happiness or unhappiness, residence abroad, splendour, strength and weakness are the products, say the wise astrologers, of the 1st bhava.

->NOTES.<-

This sloka appears in जातिकरत The third पाद (Pada) reads thus :
प्रवासचिन्तानलदुर्बलानि.

नरश्चिरायुर्नृपपूजितः सुखी लग्नं भवेत् सौम्यगृहं यथा तथा ।
लग्नं यदि स्वामिनिरीक्षितं धनी कुशाग्रबुद्धिः कुलकीर्तिवर्द्धनः १४

*Sloka 14.* The more auspicious the rising sign is at a person's birth, the longer will he live; the more will he command the respect of the rulers and the happier in consequence will he be. And if the rising sign be also aspected by its lord, he will be a master of riches, quite a genius and a great credit to his family.

->NOTES.<-

The following additional information regarding length of life is extracted from उत्तरपाराशर

लग्ने लग्नेशाचन्द्रे शशिभवनपतौ स्वोच्चगे वापि मित्रे
नायुः पूर्णं च तस्मिन्नुदितमतिशुभैर्वीक्षिते वापि युक्ते ।
किञ्चिूनं यदेकं त्वशुभग्रहयुतं मध्यमआयुः समत्वे
ह्यत्यल्पायुस्तथोक्तं वदमतित्रिबुधैः पापबाहुल्ययोगात् ॥
लग्नाटाधीशकृत्तौ स्वभवनसुहृदन्योम्यतुङ्गाधिरूढौ
दृष्टौ वा शोभनेनाप्यथ यदि मिलितौ पूर्णमायुः शुभेन ।
तत्तद्भावेशजातिद्युतिसुखतनुवर्णादिभाः संपदः स्युः

<vocab>Lagna-bhava-phalam, Jataka Parijata, Sloka, Uttara-Parashara, Bhava, Pada, Lagna, astrology</vocab>

साम्येऽल्पं पापिनः स्यात्फलमशुभबहुत्वे फलं वैपरीत्यम् ॥
लग्नेशे परमोच्चगे शुभयुते लग्ने च तद्वीक्षिते
चान्द्रेयादिशुभेन भीसितयुते संपूर्णमायुर्वदेत् ।
न्यूने पापविमिश्रमेकभवनं चेदर्द्धमध्यं द्वयो-
स्तुल्यत्वे बहुषु ग्रहेष्वशुभदं स्वल्पं तदायुर्नृणाम् ॥
परमोच्चगते विलग्ननाथे तनुभावे शुभखेचरेण दृष्टे ।
उडुपै शुभसंयुते च दृष्टे शतमायुः प्रवदन्ति बुद्धिमन्तः ।
लग्नाधीशस्य तु शनिः शत्रुश्चाल्पायुरादिशेत् ।
समत्वे मध्यमं प्रोक्तं मित्रे दीर्घायुरादिशेत् ॥
मन्देन्द्वष्टमकर्मलग्नभवनाधीशा मिथश्चोच्चगाः
मित्रस्थानगता विलोकितगृहा दृष्टाः शुभैर्वा युताः ।
आयुः पुष्कलमीपदूनमशुभैरेको युतः पापिनां
साम्ये मध्यममायुरल्पमधिकैः पापैर्वदन्ति क्रमात् ॥
लग्नेशे बलवान्दिवाकरसुतः स्यान्त्योन्यमित्रोच्चगौ
तौ पापैरवलोकिताववियुतौ चान्द्रं च लग्नं तथा ।
एतेषां च बलावलं समवलोक्य्यायुः परीक्षार्थिनः
त्वायुर्मध्यममित्युदारतनयो विन्दन्ति जातस्य हि ॥
उत्तमं मित्रवर्गं स्यात्समशत्रु च मध्यमम् ।
अधमं शत्रुमितृत्वं शत्रुवर्गन्तु मारणम् ॥

---

**लग्नांशपाद्यैर्यद्युतग्रहाद्वा तनौ स्वरूपाकृतिलक्षणानि ।**
**वर्णं वदेच्चान्द्रनवांशनाथाद्विलग्नतःसर्वशुभाशुभानि ॥ १५ ॥**

**Sloka 15.** An astrologer may declare the nature and peculiar features of a person from the lord of the rising Navamsa at his birth or from a powerful planet occupying the Lagna. His hue should be guessed from the lord of the Navamsa occupied by the Moon. All

<vocab>लग्नांश (Lagnamsa), नववांश (Navamsa), लग्नेश (Lagnathesa), आयुः (Ayu/Longevity), शनिः (Shani/Saturn), दिवाकरसुतः (Divakarasuta/Saturn), स्वरूपाकृतिलक्षण (Svarupakriti-lakshana)</vocab>

good and evil in the life of the person is to be ascertain-
ed through the Lagna.

### NOTES.
*cf. III—76 supra.*

त्रिकोणकेन्द्रे यदि लग्नाथे शुभान्विते शोभनवीक्षिते वा ।
शुभग्रहागारगते बलाढ्ये चतुःसमुद्रान्तयशः समेति ॥ १६ ॥

*Sloka 16.* If at a person's birth the lord of the rising sign being in great strength in the house of a benefic planet occupy a Kendra or Trikona position from the Lagna and be aspected by or associated with auspicious planets, the fame of the person born will extend up to the four oceans.

### NOTES.
This sloka appears in जातक रत्न. The reading there is शुभग्रहांशगते for शुभग्रहागारगते.

*cf. also the following sloka from the same work.*

लग्नाधिपे शुभयुते यदि तुङ्गभागे केन्द्रत्रिकोणसहिते शुभदृष्टियुक्ते ।
कर्माधिपेन सहिते यदि वा स्वगेहे सद्भावकीर्तिधनधान्यचिरायुुरेति ॥

होराधिनाथे रिपुरन्ध्ररिःफे पापान्विते पापनिरोक्षिते वा ।
पापग्रहाणां भवनोपयाते जातोऽप्रकाशो भवतीह मर्त्यः ॥ १७ ॥

*Sloka 17.* A person becomes obscure if, at his birth, the lord of the rising sign being in the house of a malefic planet, occupies the 6th, 8th or 12th place from the Lagna and is aspected by or associated with a malignant planet.

### NOTES.
1. For the first पाद, जातकरत्न reads लग्नेश्वरे रन्ध्रगते च दृष्टे.

कीर्तिस्थानपतौ विलग्नभवने जातः स्वयं कीर्तिमान्
वित्तस्थे तु विशेषकीर्तिसहितः स्वोच्चादिवर्गान्विते ।

<vocab>Lagna, Sloka, Kendra, Trikona, benefic planet, malefic planet, Jataka Parijata, Jatakaratna, horoscopy, astrology</vocab>

<header>Sl. 18-19 एकादशोऽध्यायः 717</header>

## दुःखैश्चञ्चलयात्रया हततनुर्जातोऽथवा दुर्जनः
## केन्द्रे कोणगते शुभग्रहयुते यात्रासुखं जायते ॥ १८ ॥

*Sloka 18.* If the lord of the कीर्तिभाव (Keerthibhava), *i.e.* the 10th be in the Lagna, the person born will become famous. If the planet be in the 2nd bhava and have attained its exaltation and other benefic vargas he will have remarkable celebrity. If the planet be in a bad place, he will be knocked up by ceaseless travelling or will be a knave. But if the planet occupy a Kendra or Trikona in conjunction with a benefic planet, the person will have ease in travelling.

->NOTES.<-

This sloka finds a place in जातकरत्न.

## होरास्वामिनि पञ्चमे यदि सुतस्थानेशयुक्तेऽथवा
## भाग्ये वा यदि भाग्यपेन सहिते लग्ने यशस्वी पिता ।
## भ्रातृस्थानपतौ विलग्नगृहगे तत्कारकभ्रातृपा-
## वेकस्यौ बलशालिनौ च यदि वा तत्सोदरः कीर्तिमान् १९

*Sloka 19.* If the lord of the 1st bhava occupy the 5th or the 9th or being in conjunction with the lord of the 5th or the 9th occupy the Lagna, the father of the person born will be a famous man. If the lord of the 3rd bhava be in the Lagna or the कारक (Karaka-Mars) as well as the lord of the भ्रातृभाव (Bhratrubhava) be together in one house and possess strength, a brother of the person born will become renowned.

->NOTES.<-

For the latter half of this sloka, जातकरत्न has the following :

## लग्ने स्थिरभे शुभग्रहयुते सौम्यग्रहैर्वर्गे
## जातः सर्वसुखं धनं च बहुलं संप्राप्नुयात्तैर्युतः ॥

<vocab>Keerthibhava, Lagna, bhava, Kendra, Trikona, Jatakaratna, Bhratrubhava, Karaka, vargas</vocab>

->विद्याधिपे वा यदि चन्द्रसूनौ लग्ने सुखे लग्नपसंयुते वा ।<-
->बलान्विते पापदृशा विहीने विद्यायशस्वी भवति प्रजातः ॥२०॥<-

*Sloka 20* When the lord of the 2nd bhava or Mercury is in the Lagna or when the 2nd bhava is occupied by the lord of the Lagna and when the bhava in either case is strong and free from malefic aspect, the person born will become famous for his learning.

---

->NOTES.<-

विद्याधिप (Vidyadhipa) here means the lord of the 2nd bhava; (*vide sloka 49 infra*) and not that of the 4th bhava referred to in XII—59 *infra*.

The same remark applies to the term सुख.

---

->धर्मोदयेशौ नवमोदयस्थौ धर्मोदयस्थौ तनुधर्मपौ वा ।<-
->सुरेन्द्रवन्द्येक्षितसंयुतौ चेत् समेति जातश्चिरकालभाग्यम् ॥२१॥<-

*Sloka 21.* If the lords of the 9th and the 1st bhavas from the Lagna be in their respective bhavas or if the lords of the 1st and the 9th occupy the 9th and the 1st respectively, and if the planets in their several positions be aspected by or associated with Jupiter, the person born will enjoy good luck for a long time.

---

->लग्नाधिपस्य व्ययगे तदुच्चमित्रे सुहृत्तुङ्गस्वराशिगे वा ।<-
->तद्राशिगे वा यदि मित्रखेटे तस्य स्थितिर्जन्मवसुन्धरायाम् २२<-

*Sloka 22.* A person will abide in the place of his birth when a planet occupying the 12th place from that of the lord of the Lagna happens to be in exaltation or in a friendly house or is aspected by a planet that is friendly or is in exaltation or when that Rashi is occupied by a friendly planet.

---

->लग्नेशो व्ययपे विलग्नपरिपौ नीचोऽथवा दुर्बले<-
->जातो याति विदेशमिन्दुजाचार्येण दृष्टे यदि।<-

<vocab>Vidyadhipa, bhava, Lagna, Mercury, Jupiter, Rashi, Jataka Parijata</vocab>

--- Page 98 ---
<lang>sa</lang>
<page-num>710</page-num>
<header>Sl.-23-25 एकादशोऽध्यायः 710</header>

->तत्रैव स्थितिरन्त्यपे रविकरच्छन्ने विलग्नाधिपा-<-
->दल्पग्रामचरो बलिन्यपि धनग्रामाधिवासो भवेत् ॥ २३ ॥<-

> *Sloka 23.* When the lord of the 12th house from that occupied by the lord of the <term>Lagna</term> is the latter's enemy or is in depression or weak, the person born goes to a foreign country. He will abide permanently there if the व्ययप (<term>Vyayapa</term>) referred to above be aspected by Venus in the capacity of a friend. His haunt will be a small township if the same व्ययप (<term>Vyayapa</term>) be eclipsed by the Sun; if the व्ययप (<term>Vyayapa</term>) be possessed of strength, the haunt will be a wealthy municipality.

---

->होरेशाद्व्ययपे विलग्नभवनात् केन्द्रत्रिकोणस्थिते<-
->मित्रस्वोच्चगृहोपगे शुभयुते पार्श्वद्वये मानवः ।<-
->चेतोरम्यमहीचरो दितिचराचार्येन्दुशुक्रैक्षिते<-
->दिव्यक्षेत्रमुपैति जन्मधरणीवासस्तदर्थे रिपौ ॥ २४ ॥<-

> *Sloka 24.* When the lord of the 12th place from that occupied by the lord of the <term>lagna</term> is in a <term>Kendra</term> or <term>Trikona</term> from the 1st <term>bhava</term>, and in a <term>Rasi</term> which is a friend's house, its own or its exaltation sign and has auspicious planets on each side, the person born will be a sojourner in regions pleasing to the heart. If the व्ययप (<term>Vyayapa</term>) referred to above be aspected by <term>Jupiter</term>, the <term>Moon</term> or <term>Venus</term>, he goes to a charmingly beautiful land; but if this व्ययप (<term>Vyayapa</term>) occupy the 2nd place from the lord of the <term>Lagna</term> and be unfriendly to the latter, the person will abide in his native land.

---

->विदेशभाग्यं चरमे विलग्ने चरे तदीशे चरखेटदृष्टे ।<-
->स्थिरे स्वदेशे बहुभाग्ययुक्तः स्थिरग्रहैर्भूरिधनान्वितः स्यात् ॥ २५ ॥<-

> *Sloka 25.* When a moveable sign is the <term>Lagna</term>

<vocab>Lagna, Vyayapa, Kendra, Trikona, Bhava, Rasi, Jupiter, Moon, Venus, Sun, Horoscope, Astrology</vocab>

--- Page 99 ---
<page-num>720</page-num>
<header>जातकपारिजाते . Adh. XI.</header>
<lang>sa, en</lang>

and its lord is in a <term>moveable</term> <term>Rasi</term> and aspected by planets occupying <term>moveable</term> <term>Rasis</term>, the person born will have his fortune in a foreign country. When an <term>immoveable</term> sign is the <term>Lagna</term> and its lord is also in an <term>immoveable</term> sign and aspected by planets occupying <term>immoveable</term> signs, the person will be in his own country, exceedingly prosperous and possessed of abundant wealth.

->NOTES.<-

This <term>sloka</term> appears also in <term>जातक रत्न</term>.

होराधीशे पापखेटे रिपुस्थे पापक्षेत्रे भानुपुत्रेण युक्ते ।
शूद्रप्रायो राहुणा केतुना वा जातश्चाण्डालोऽथवा नीचतुल्यः ॥२६॥

*Sloka 26.* When the lord of the <term>Lagna</term> is a malefic planet and occupies the 6th place from the <term>Lagna</term> being associated with <term>Saturn</term> in a malefic sign, the person born will be like a <term>Sudra</term>. And if <term>Rahu</term> or <term>Ketu</term> take the place of <term>Saturn</term> in the above, the person will be a <term>Chandala</term> or some equally low person in his habits.

लग्नाधिपे शोभनराशिभुक्ते बलोपयावे तनुपुष्टिमेति ।
लग्नस्थखेटे रिपुनीचभागे दुःस्थानपे देहसुखं न याति ॥ २७ ॥

*Sloka 27.* When the lord of the <term>Lagna</term> is in an auspicious sign and is possessed of <term>strengh</term>, the person born will be in good ease having a command of all bodily comforts. When the planet in the <term>Lagna</term> is in a depression or inimical <term>Navamsa</term> and happens to be the lord of a <term>दुःस्थान</term> (<term>Dusthbana</term>), the person born will lack bodily comforts.

<margin>cf. जातक रत्न</margin>
लग्नेश्वरः क्रूरसमन्वितश्चेज्जातस्य सौख्यादि विनाशमेति ।
तादृक्फले नारागतेऽपि तस्य शुभेसितश्चेत्फलमन्यथा स्यात् ॥

<vocab>Jataka Parijata, Lagna, Rasi, Navamsa, Dusthbana, Sudra, Rahu, Ketu, Chandala, Saturn, Adh. XI</vocab>

--- Page 100 ---
<lang>Sanskrit, English</lang>
<page-num>721</page-num>
<header>SL 28-30 एकादशोऽध्यायः</header>

विलग्नदर्शी तनुपो विलग्नगस्त्रयोऽपि षष्ठाष्टमरिःफराशिपाः ।
सपत्ननीचोपगताश्च दुर्बला यदि स्वपाके न फलं प्रकुर्युः ॥ २८ ॥

*Sloka 28.* If the three planets owning the 6th, the 8th and the 12th bhavas from the Lagna be connected with the Lagna, one of them aspecting it, another owning it and the third occupying it, and if they at the same time be in their inimical or depression signs devoid of all strength, they can do no good at the time of the ripening of their several dasa periods.

लग्ने जलर्क्षे शुभखैचरैर्युक्ते तनोः स्थौल्यमुदाहरन्ति ।
लग्नाधिपे तोयखगे बलाढ्ये सौम्यान्विते तत्तनुपुष्टिमाहुः ॥ २९ ॥

*Sloka 29.* When the Lagna is a watery sign and is occupied by benefic planets, astrologers declare stoutness of person as the effect thereof. When the lord of the Lagna occupies a watery sign and is in conjunction with benefic planets, the person born, say the astrologers, will have a sound constitution.

->centered text<-
NOTES.
->centered text<-

तोयगते is the reading adopted in जातकतत्त्व for तोयखगे in the 3rd पाद of this sloka.

लग्नाधिपे नाशगते तु शुष्कराशौ तनोः कष्टमतिव कृच्छ्रम् ।
लग्नाधिपस्यांशपराशिनाथः शुष्कग्रहः स्यात्तनुशुष्कमाहुः ॥ ३० ॥

*Sloka 30.* When the lord of the Lagna is in the 8th bhava and in a dry sign, astrologers say that the person born will have great bodily suffering. If the lord of the Rasi in which is the planet owning the Navamsa occupied by the lord of the Lagna be a planet owning a dry sign, the effect will be emaciation.

->centered text<-
NOTES.
->centered text<-

Some books read लग्नांशपस्वांशपराशिनाथः

<sig>91</sig>

<vocab>Lagna, bhava, dasa, Sloka, Rasi, Navamsa, watery sign, dry sign, emaciation, Jataka Tattva, stoutness</vocab>

<header>जातकपारिजाते Adh. XI.</header>

->मध्यस्थे रिपुखेटयोस्तनुपतौ जातोऽरिभीतो भवेत्<-
->केतौ लग्नगतेऽथवा फणिपतौ दुःस्थे विलग्नाधिपे ।<-
->तत्पाके तदरीशभुक्तिसमये वैकल्यमङ्गे वदेत्<-
->लग्ने शत्रुपतौ फणिराजयुते देहव्रणं देहिनाम् ॥ ३१ ॥<-

**Sloka 31.** When the lord of the Lagna is between two inimical planets, the person born will be in dread of enemies. If Ketu or Rahu be in the Lagna and the lord thereof be in a <term>दुःस्थान</term> (<term>Dusthana</term> 6th, 8th or 12th), the person will suffer privation of some limb in the ripening of the <term>dasa</term> of the lord of the Lagna and in the <term>antardasa</term> of the lord of the 6th house (from the lord of the Lagna). If the lord of the 6th house from the Lagna occupy it in conjunction with Rahu or Ketu, an ulcer will break out in the body.

->NOTES.<-

This appears in <term>जातकभरण</term>. In the 3rd पाद, <term>तदधीश</term> is the reading adopted there for <term>तदरीश</term> in the text.

---

->खलैर्विहीने यदि लग्ननाथे केन्द्रत्रिकोणे सति रोगभाक् स्यात् ।<-
->लग्नेश्वराधिष्ठितराशिनाथे दुःस्थानगे दुर्बलदेहवान् स्यात् ॥ ३२ ॥<-

**Sloka 32.** If the lord of the Lagna occupying a <term>Trikona</term> or a <term>Kendra</term> be devoid of strength, the person born will be ailing. If the lord of the house occupied by the lord of the Lagna be in a <term>दुःस्थान</term> (<term>Dusthana</term>), he will have a weak constitution.

->NOTES.<-

(First half). The following <term>yoga</term> leading to the same effect is given in <term>जातकभरण</term>.

> ->दुःस्थानपेनापि युतो विलग्ननाथो विलग्ने स तु रोगभाक् स्यात् ॥<-

---

->सक्रूरो देहपो देहसौख्यहान्त्यारिन्ध्रगः ।<-
->सार्कीशे देहपे दुःस्थे लग्नस्थे चाशु रोगवान् ॥ ३३ ॥<-

<vocab>जातकपारिजाते, Lagna, Ketu, Rahu, Dusthana, dasa, antardasa, Trikona, Kendra, yoga, जातकभरण</vocab>

--- Page 102 ---
<lang>Sanskrit, English</lang>
<page-num>729</page-num>
<header>Sl. 33-35 एकादशोऽध्यायः 729</header>

**Sloka 33.** The lord of the Lagna occupying the 12th, 6th or 8th bhava (from the Lagna) in conjunction with a malefic planet destroys the bodily health of the person born. He will be ailing if the lord of the 1st bhava in conjunction with the lord of the 6th occupies a दुःस्थान (Dusthhana) or the Lagna.

->लग्ने सपापे लग्नेशे बलहीनेऽपि रोगवान् ।<-
->लग्नेशे दुर्बलो कोपी निर्व्याधिः केन्द्रकोणगे ॥ ३४ ॥<-

**Sloka 31.** When a malefic planet is in the Lagna and the lord thereof is without strength, the person born will be sickly. He will be of a fretful temper if the lord of the Lagna be weak; exempt from ailment if the lord of the Lagna be in a Kendra or Trikona.
<note>The number "31" in the original text is likely a printer's error for "34" based on the preceding Sanskrit verse numbering.</note>

->देहेष्टस्थितराशीशे नाशगे दुर्बलो भवेत् ।<-
->भावेशाक्रान्तराशीशैर्दुःस्थैर्भावाश्च दुर्बलाः ॥ ३५ ॥<-

**Sloka 35.** If the lord of the sign occupied by the lord of the Lagna be in the 8th place from the Lagna, the person will be weak. Bhavas generally become weak when their lords are in Rasis of which the owners are badly placed (i.e. are in दुःस्थान Duhsthana).

->सर्पारियोर्विद्विजौ योगे वा सर्पमन्दयोः ।<-
->लग्ने कुजे नाभिगुल्फवृषणस्थौल्यमादिशेत् ॥ ३६ ॥<-

**Sloka 36.** A person has an enlarged scrotum as the consequence of the conjunction of Rahu and Mars or of Rahu and Saturn. When Mars occupies the Lagna, an astrologer may predict swelling in the navel, ankle and in the testicles.

->लग्नेशे यदि रिःफगे दिनकरस्यारातिनाथान्विते<-
->जायावान् बहुरोमवान् कृशतनुः संरक्तगौरद्युतिः ।<-

<vocab>Lagna, bhava, malefic, Dusthhana, Sloka, Kendra, Trikona, Rahu, Mars, Saturn, scrotum, Rasis</vocab>

<lang>Sanskrit, English</lang>

<header>जातकपारिजाते Adh. XI.</header>

->लग्नर्क्षादरिनाथपेन च युते जातोऽसिताङ्गो यदा ।<-
->साहौ लग्नपतौ तु शस्त्रनविषादङ्गीर्ति समेति ध्रुवम् ॥ ३७ ॥<-

**Sloka 37.** If the lord of the Lagna occupying the 12th bhava be in conjunction with the lord of the 6th bhava from that occupied by the Sun, the person born will get readily married, be characterized by much hair about his person, have a slim figure and a hue composed of red and white. If the lord of the 6th bhava from the Lagna be also in conjunction with the lord of the Lagna in the above position, the person born will be dark in personal appearance; but if <term>Rahu</term> be with the lord of the Lagna, the person concerned will surely run the risk of treachery or poison.

---

->लग्ने शुभे शोभनदृष्टियुक्ते बाल्यात्सुखं तन्नहि पापयोगात् ।<-
->दुःखी भवेत्पापबहुत्वयोगे लग्ने तु बाल्यान्मरणान्तमाहुः ॥ ३८ ॥<-

**Sloka 38.** A person will be happy from his childhood when the <term>Lagna</term> is auspicious and is aspected by <term>benefic planets</term> provided there are no <term>malefic ones</term> in the Lagna. But if several malefic planets should be in the Lagna, the person born will be unhappy from first to last.

---

->देवलोकांशगे शुक्रे लग्नेशे गोपुरांशके ।<-
->लग्ने शुभग्रहैर्दृष्टे मध्यान्ते सौख्यमाप्नुयात् ॥ ३९ ॥<-

**Sloka 39.** A person will be happy in the middle and concluding portions of his life if, at the time of his birth, <term>Venus</term> has attained <term>Devalokamsa</term> (*vide Adhyaya I. Sl. 46*), the lord of the Lagna a <term>Gopuramsa</term> (*vide Adhyaya I. Sl. 45*) and benefic planets aspect the rising sign.

<vocab>Jataka Parijata, Lagna, Bhava, Rahu, Devalokamsa, Gopuramsa, benefic planets, malefic planets, Sanskrit, astrology</vocab>

<lang>Sanskrit, English</lang>

<header>Sl. 40-44 एकादशोऽध्यायः</header>

->लग्ने शुभे धने पापे केन्द्रे पापसमन्विते ।<-
->लग्नेश्वरे तूत्तमांशे चादौ दुःखं ततः सुखम् ॥ ४० ॥<-

*Sloka 40.* When there is a benefic planet in the 1st <term>bhava</term>, but a malefic one in the 2nd as well as in a <term>Kendra</term>, and when the lord of the <term>Lagna</term> has attained the <term>Uttamamsa</term>, the person born suffers misery in early life and is happy thereafter.

---

->लग्नेशे शुभराशिस्थे शुभग्रहनिरीक्षिते ।<-
->गोपुरांशगते वाऽपि षोडशाब्दात्परं सुखम् ॥ ४१ ॥<-

*Sloka 41.* When the lord of the <term>Lagna</term> occupies a benefic sign and is aspected by a benefic planet or has attained a <term>Gopuramsa</term>, the person born will be happy after his 16th year.

---

->लग्नेशस्यांशनाथे तु केन्द्रकोणोच्चसंयुते ।<-
->लाभे वा बलसंयुक्ते त्रिंशद्वर्षात्परं सुखम् ॥ ४२ ॥<-

*Sloka 42.* When the planet owning the <term>Navamsa</term> occupied by the lord of the <term>Lagna</term> is in a <term>Kendra</term>, a <term>Trikona</term> or exaltation, or is in the 11th <term>bhava</term> possessed of strength, the person born will be happy after the first 30 years of his life.

---

->लग्ने रव्यादिसंदृष्टे पादशः फलमुच्यते ।<-
->राजसेवी पितृधनो जलपण्यो महाधनः ॥ ४३ ॥<-
->धार्मिकः स्थूलशिश्रः स्याद्विद्याशिल्पयशोन्वितः ।<-
->राजपूज्यो व्रतयुतो वेश्यासक्तो धनी सुखी ॥ ४४ ॥<-

*Slokas 43-44.* When the <term>Lagna</term> is aspected by the Sun and other planets, the effect in the several cases on the person born is given in order in the sloka-quarters that follow: If the Sun be the aspecting planet, the

<vocab>Lagna, bhava, Kendra, Uttamamsa, Gopuramsa, Navamsa, Trikona, aspected, benefic, malefic</vocab>

person born will serve a king and inherit wealth from his father ; if the Moon, he will engage in sea-borne trade and be opulent ; if Mars, he will be virtuous and have a big *membrum virile* ; if Mercury, he will have learning, artistic skill and fame ; if Jupiter, he will be honored by his sovereign and devoutly observant of religious vows ; if Venus, he will be addicted to women of ill-fame, opulent and voluptuous.

->मन्ददृष्टे विलग्ने तु वृद्धस्त्रीको मली खलः ।<-
->केनाप्यदृष्टं लग्नं चेद्राशिग्रहवशाद्वदेत् ॥ ४५ ॥<-

Sloka 45. If Saturn aspects the <term>Lagna</term>, he will have aged wives, will be unclean and be mischief-making. If no planet aspect the <term>Lagna</term>, the astrologer should make his prediction in accordance with the planet connected with the लग्नराशि (<term>Lagna Rasi</term>).

->लग्ने स्वामीक्षिते राजा तत्प्रियो वा धनी सुखी ।<-
->सौम्येक्षितेऽखिलं सौम्यं पापदृष्टे त्वशोभनम् ॥ ४६ ॥<-

Sloka 46. When the <term>Lagna</term> is aspected by its lord, the person born will be either a king or his favorite, wealthy and well-off. If the <term>Lagna</term> be aspected by an auspicious planet, all will be auspicious. If by an evil planet, everything will turn out ill.

->सुखी लग्ने द्वादिदृष्टे सर्वदृष्टे नृपो भवेत् ।<-
->लग्ने त्रयः शुभा राजा दुःखी पापग्रहान्वयः ॥ ४७ ॥<-

Sloka 47. When the <term>Lagna</term> is aspected by two or more benefic planets, the person born is happy. When it is aspected by all the benefic planets, he will be a king. When there are three benefic planets in the <term>Lagna</term>, the effect on the person born is, he will make a good king.

<vocab>Jataka Parijata, Lagna, Lagna Rasi, Sloka, benefic planets, aspected, Saturn, Moon, Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus, membrum virile</vocab>

<lang>Sanskrit, English</lang>

<header>Sl. 48-50 एकादशोऽध्यायः 727</header>
<warning>The image is a slightly aged print with some ink bleeding and minor blurriness in the Devanagari script.</warning>

If there be three malefic planets in the Lagna, he will be unhappy.

> लग्नाधिपोऽतिबलवानशुभैरदृष्टः
> केन्द्रस्थितः शुभखगैरवलोक्यमानः ।
> मृत्युं विधूय विदधाति स दीर्घमायुः
> सार्धं गुणैर्बहुभिरूर्जितराजलक्ष्म्या ॥ ४८ ॥

*Sloka 48.* The lord of the rising sign when possessed of great strength and unaspected by malefic planets, but aspected by benefic ones and occupying a <term>Kendra</term> position wards off death and secures to the child long life graced with the strong virtues of a vigorous sovereignty.

->NOTES.<-

See Adhyaya IV. Sloka 83, *supra*.

## ॥ अथ द्वितीयभावफलम् ॥

> वित्तं नेत्रं सुखं विद्या वाक्कुटुम्बाशनानि च ।
> द्वितीयस्थानजन्यानि क्रमाज्ज्योतिर्विदो विदुः ॥ ४९ ॥

*Sloka 49.* Wealth, visual power, ease, learning, speech, household and food—these, the astrologers recognise as regularly derivable from the 2nd <term>bhava</term>.

> वित्तायोदयराशयः पतिंयुता वित्ताधिको जायते
> लाभस्थौ धनलाभपौ निजसुहृत्तुङ्गादिगौ चेत्तथा ।
> सद्वर्गामघनाधिपौ तनुगतावन्योन्यमिश्रग्रहौ
> लग्नेशे धनलाभराशिप्रयुते लग्ने बहुद्रव्यवान् ॥ ५० ॥

*Sloka 50.* A person will have exceeding wealth (1) when the 2nd, the 11th and the 1st bhavas in his horoscope are occupied by their lords ; (2) when the

lords of the 2nd and the 11th bhavas are in the 11th bhava and happen to be in **स्वक्षेत्र** (**Swakshetra**), **मित्रक्षेत्र** (**Mithrakshetra**) or in exaltation; (3) when the lords of the 11th and the 2nd bhavas, being friendly planets, occupy the Lagna and lastly (4) when the lord of the Lagna is in it in conjunction with the lords of the 2nd and the 11th bhavas.

->NOTES.<-

This sloka is also in **जातकत्तत्व**.
The following wealth-producing yogas are extracted from
(1) **उत्तरपाराशर**

> भावैश्चतुर्भिर्बलिभिर्धनभाग्यायकर्म्मभिः ।
> संपूर्णवित्तः सततं कुबेर इव जायते ॥
> कर्म्मायस्वशुभेश्वरा रिपुमृतिप्रान्त्यान्विहाय स्थिता
> लग्नाद्वा यदि वा मिथोपि धनिको जायेत मर्त्यः शतैः ।
> साहस्रैरपि चेद्विधायमयुतैः सम्बन्धिताश्चेन्मिथः
> स्वक्षेत्रोच्चपरस्पराश्रयतया लक्षैश्च कोट्यापि वा ॥
> धनेशाधिपती परस्परसुहृत्स्वतुङ्गस्थितौ
> शुभेन यदि वीक्षितौ शुभयुतौ धनं पुष्कलम् ।
> ऋतं मधुरया गिरा वरकुटुम्बमेतद्भवे-
> दत्तदिरपि मिश्रिते तदधिके फलं पूर्ववत् ॥
> कर्म्मायस्वशुभेश्वरा रिपुमृतिप्रान्तेषु लग्नस्य वा
> यद्वान्योन्यमकिञ्चनो भवतु ते स्वायत्त्रिगा द्रव्यवान् ।
> संख्यायां यदि ते पणैश्च नवभिः केन्द्रत्रिकोणस्थिता
> श्वैक्तरैस्तावदुदीर्यते नवतिभिस्ते तु प्रसक्ताः स्त्रियः ॥
> मितस्वस्थितयो यदा बहुशतैरुच्चस्वभावस्थिता
> साहस्रैरयुताधिपोपि मिलितास्तत्रैव लक्षाधिपाः ।
> अन्योम्योच्चसुहृन्निजबलिनः कोट्यादिभिः संख्यया

<header>Sl. 51 एकादशोऽध्यायः</header>

->जायेत्तैव स भूतले खलु पुमान् योगैश्च सर्वोन्नतैः ॥<-
->धनेशः पापी चेद्भवति हि यदा तस्य तु दशा<-
->तदा राहुकार्किक्षितितनयमुक्तौ धनपतिः ।<-
->निरोधः पापेनाप्युत धनपतिस्त्योपि सहितो<-
->धने वा पापी स्यादनुक्लन्निरोधैर्यविहिनिः ॥<-

### (2) जातकरण

लग्नेश्वरस्यांशपतिस्वराशिनाथस्थितांशपती बळाढ्ये ।
वैशेषिकांशे यदि तुङ्गभागे धनाधिपे त्वर्थवतां वरोऽसौ ॥
लग्नेश्वरेण सहितं धनराशिनाथे केन्द्रस्थिते परमतुङ्ग नवांशके च ।
वैशेषिकांशकयुते शुभदृष्टियुक्ते योगे बहुत्वधनलाभमिहाहुरायार्याः ॥
वित्तेश्वरेण सहितं भवराशिनाथे कर्माधिपस्यनवभागपददृष्टियुक्ते ।
वैशेषिकांशकयुते परमोच्चभागे योगे बहुत्वधनलाभमिहाहुरायार्याः ॥
कर्माधिपेन सहितं धनराशिनाथे लग्नेश्वरस्थितनवांशपतीश्वरेण ।
दृष्टे चतुष्टयगते शुभदृष्टियोगे जातो बहुत्वधनलाभमुपैति बाल्ये ॥
लग्नायलाभभवने शुभखेटेवन्द्रे ह्युच्चे सुहृत्स्वगृहगेत्र विशेषिकांशे ।
तुङ्गांशगे धनपतिस्त्वनवांशनाथे दृष्टे बहुत्वधनलाभमुपैति बाल्ये ॥
लग्नेश्वर धनगते धनपस्तु लाभे लाभेश्वर धनगते बलपूर्णयुक्ते ।
स्वोच्चस्वमित्रभवने शुभमंयुते वा जातः स्वकीयधनमेति बहुप्रकारैः ॥
सर्वग्रहेष्वतिबली यदि लग्ननाथः केन्द्रस्थितस्त्रिदशपूजितसंयुतश्च ।
वैशेषिकांशकयुते धनराशिनाथे वित्तं स्वकीयबलतोर्जितमाहुरायार्याः ॥
लग्नेशसंयुतनवांशपतिस्वराशिनाथो बलेन सहितो धनपस्तु मित्रः ।
केन्द्रत्रिकोणसहिते स्वगृहेयवापि जातः स्वकीयधनमेति बहुप्रकारैः ॥

धनोपयातः प्रथमोर्थदर्शी ग्रहो द्वितीयो धनपस्तृतीयः ।
तत्पार्कमुक्तौ धनलाभमेति क्रमेण तत्कारवर्गमूलात् ॥ ५१ ॥

**Sloka 51.** Note first the planet occupying the 2nd
bhava; next, the planet aspecting that bhava; thirdly,

<sig>92</sig>
<meta>image quality is good but slightly grainy; catchword/signature 92 at bottom center; Sanskrit text is in Devanagari script; contains astrological verses (Shlokas) regarding wealth.</meta>
<vocab>एकादशोऽध्यायः, जातकरण, लग्नेश्वर, वैशेषिकांश, धनराशिनाथ, धनलाभ, bhava, Sloka</vocab>

<header>जातकपारिजाते Adh. XI.</header>
<meta>Clean digital scan of a printed Sanskrit-English text. Horizontal rules separate sections.</meta>

the planet owning it. In the ripening of the dasas of these planets will the person concerned get his wealth through a source indicated by the <term>Varga</term> attained by the धनकारक (<term>Dhanakaraka</term>).

---

->धनस्थे यदि लग्नेशे निधिमान् बलसंयुते ।<-
->दुर्बले पापसंयुक्ते वंचनादिफलं वदेत् ॥ ५२ ॥<-

*Sloka 52.* When the lord of the <term>Lagna</term> occupies the 2nd <term>bhava</term> in strength, the person born will possess a treasure. If the planet in question be weak and associated with a malefic planet, the person concerned will suffer the consequence of deception or other trick practised upon him.

---

->धनस्थितः पापदृशा समेतः सपत्नीचार्ककराभितप्तः ।<-
->तत्पाकभुक्तौ धननाशमाहुः स गोचरे दुष्टबलान्वितो वा ॥५३॥<-

*Sloka 53.* When the planet occupying the 2nd <term>bhava</term> is aspected by a malefic planet and is unfriendly to the lord of that <term>bhava</term> or in depression or eclipsed by the Sun's rays, the person born will suffer pecuniary loss in the ripening of the <term>dasa</term> of that planet. The same thing happens when the planet in question in its progress through the zodiac becomes powerful for evil.

---

->कुटुम्बभावे बहुखेटयुक्ते धनप्रदव्योमचरे बलाढ्ये ।<-
->स्वतुङ्गमित्रस्वगृहोपगे वा धनं समेत्यामरणान्तमाहुः ॥ ५४ ॥<-

*Sloka 54.* When the 2nd <term>bhava</term> is occupied by many (benefic) planets and the धनकारक (<term>Dhanakaraka</term>) is possessed of strength or occupies its exaltation, a friendly sign or its own house, the person born, say the astrologers, will be getting wealth till the moment of his death.

<vocab>Jataka Parijata, Sloka, Lagna, bhava, Dhanakaraka, Varga, dasas, malefic, benefic, zodiac, pecuniary</vocab>

--- Page 110 ---
<lang>Sanskrit</lang>

<header>Sl. 55-57 एकादशोऽध्यायः</header>

---

### NOTES.

<unclear>...</unclear> धनप्रद (**Dhanaprada**) may also mean the lord of the 2nd bhava.

->वित्ताधीशे सोदरेण युक्ते लग्नास्थे च भातृवित्तं समेति ।<-
->मातृस्थानस्वामिना मातृवित्तं यद्भावेनैनान्वितॄ तद्धनं स्यात् ५५<-

*Sloka 55.* When the lord of the 2nd bhava is in the Lagna associated with the lord of the 3rd, the person born will get wealth acquired by a brother. If the lord of the 4th bhava take the place of the lord of the 3rd bhava in the above, the person concerned will inherit property from his mother. Generally, wealth comes in connection with that bhava whereof the lord occupies the Lagna in conjunction with the lord of wealth.

->वित्ताधीशे लग्ने लग्नाथे वित्तस्थानेऽयत्नतो वित्तमेव ।<-
->यद्भावस्थौ लग्नवित्तेश्वरौ चेत् तन्मूला तु द्रव्यवित्तिर्नराणाम् ५६<-

*Sloka 56.* When the lord of wealth occupies the Lagna and the lord of that bhava occupies the 2nd, wealth comes to the person born without any effort at all. Whatever bhava the lords of the 1st and 2nd bhavas may together occupy, it is through that bhava that acquisition of wealth on the part of men usually takes place.

->चन्द्र: कुटुम्बभवने शुक्रेण निरीक्षित: प्रदाता च ।<-
->सौम्ये शुभसंदृष्टे स एव धनवान् सदा ज्ञेय: ॥ ५७ ॥<-

*Sloka 57.* When the Moon occupying the 2nd bhava is aspected by Venus, the person born will be munificent If Mercury in the 2nd bhava be aspected by a benefic planet, he will have, be it remembered, wealth at his command at all times.

<vocab>bhava, Lagna, Sloka, Dhanaprada, Ekādasho'dhyāyaḥ, bhātṛ-vitta, mātṛ-vitta, dravya-vitti, Venus, Mercury, Moon, wealth</vocab>

--- Page 111 ---
<lang>san</lang>
<page-num>732</page-num>
<header>जातकपारिजाते Adh. XI.</header>

अर्थाधीशो यत्र संस्थो नराणां तस्मिन् काले तत्र वृद्धिविशेषात् ।
तद्भागे च द्रव्यलाभं करोति वक्रोऽसौ चेदिक्षु सर्वार्थसिद्धिः ॥ ५८ ॥

*Sloka 58.* Note in what quarter the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term> is; it is in that quarter chiefly that men will find their prosperity during the <term>dasa</term> period of the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term>: for when moving ordinarily the planet leads to the acquisition of wealth in the region indicated. If it be retrograde at the time of birth, there will be acquisition of every valuable thing in all quarters.

धनेशे लाभसंयुक्ते लाभेशो धनलामगे ।
तावुभौ केन्द्रगौ वापि धनवान् ख्यातिमान् भवेत् ॥ ५९ ॥

*Sloka 59.* If the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term> be in the 11th and the lord of the 11th be in the 2nd or the 11th, or if the lords of the 2nd and the 11th <term>bhavas</term> from the <term>Lagna</term> occupy a <term>Kendra</term>, the person born will be wealthy and renowned.

->योगजातक<-

धननाथे गते लाभे लाभस्वामी धनस्थितः ।
तत्रैव शुभखेटाश्च गतास्ते धनवान्यदाः ॥
धनेशे व्ययपष्ठस्थे व्ययेशे वित्तगेऽथवा ।
लाभेशे रिपुरन्ध्रस्थे व्यये वा धननाशनम् ॥ ६० ॥

*Sloka 60.* If the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term> be in the 12th or the 6th, or the lord of the 12th in the 2nd, and the planet owning the 11th occupy the 6th, the 8th or the 12th, the effect will be loss of wealth.

व्ययभावगते जीवे वित्तेशे बलवर्जिते ।
शुभैरनीक्षिते लग्ने वित्तनाशं वदेद्बुधः ॥ ६१ ॥

<vocab>जातकपारिजाते, Jatakaparijata, Adhyaya, Sloka, Bhava, Dasa, Retrograde, Lagna, Kendra, Yoga, Wealth, Jyotisha</vocab>

<lang>English, Sanskrit</lang>
<page-num>783</page-num>
<header>Sl. 61-64 एकादशोऽध्यायः 783</header>

---

*Sloka 61.* When Jupiter is in the 12th bhava and the lord of the 2nd bhava is without strength and the Lagna is not aspected by benefic planets, the astrologer should predict loss of wealth.

-> लग्नेशे धनराशिस्थे धनेशे लाभराशिगे । <-
-> लाभेशे वा विलग्नेस्थे निष्पद्यादिधनमाप्नुयात् ॥ ६२ ॥ <-

*Sloka 62.* If the lord of the Lagna occupy the 2nd bhava and the lord of the latter occupy the 11th or the lord of the 11th occupy the 1st, the person born will get treasure or other valuable property.

-> NOTES. <-

लग्नेशे च विलग्नेस्थे is another reading.

-> लग्नायधनभाग्येशाः परमोच्चांशसंयुताः । <-
-> वैशेषिकांशगा चाऽपि तदा कोटीश्वरो भवेत् ॥ ६३ ॥ <-

*Sloka 63.* When the lords of the 1st, 11th, 2nd and the 9th bhavas have attained their highest exaltation Navamsa, or Vaiseshikamsas (*vide Adhyaya I. sl. 44-47*), the person born will be lord of a crore or crores.

*Cf.* सर्वार्थचिन्तामणि
-> लग्नांशनाथभाग्येशाः परमोच्चांशसंयुताः । <-
-> वैशेषिकांशे लाभेशे तदा कोटीश्वरो भवेत् ॥ <-

-> दिनेश्वरकरच्छन्ने धनेशे नीचराशिगे । <-
-> पापषष्ट्यंशसंयुक्ते ऋणग्रस्तो भवेन्नरः ॥ ६४ ॥ <-

*Sloka 64.* When the lord of the 2nd bhava is eclipsed by the Sun's rays and in depression and in a malefic 60th portion of a sign, the person born will become plunged in debt.

-> NOTES. <-

For the same effect the following yogas quoted in जानकराज.

<vocab>bhava, Lagna, Sloka, Navamsa, Vaiseshikamsas, crore, yoga, सर्वार्थचिन्तामणि (Sarvarthachintamani), जानकराज (Janakaraja)</vocab>

<header>जातकपारिजाते Adh. XI.</header>

->पापे धने लग्नपतौ व्यये तु कर्मायपौ सूर्ययुतौ तु नीचे ।<-
->अस्तंगतौ वा शुभकर्मभेशौ ऋणादिभिर्ग्रस्तनरोत्र जातः ॥<-

->शुक्रेन्दुनयनाधीशैरेकस्थैस्तु निशान्धकः ।<-
->सूर्यशुक्रविलमेशैरदृश्यैर्मध्यलोचनः ॥ ६५ ॥<-

*Sloka 65.* A person becomes night-blind when Venus, the Moon and the lord of the 2nd bhava are together in one bhava. When the Sun, Venus and the lord of the 1st bhava are invisible (being in that portion of the zodiac which comprises the bhavas from 2 to 7, both inclusive), the person concerned will have eyes of medium visual power.

->**NOTES.**<-

This ( <unclear>क्त</unclear> in <unclear>क्तये</unclear>. ) in the light of Sloka 59 of Adhyaya 6 should be taken to refer to the 1st bhava or the Lagna.
*cf.* **जातकलंकार**
->शुक्रः सेन्दुस्त्रिस्थो जनुषि निशि नरः प्राप्नुयादन्धकत्वं<-
->जन्मान्धः सार्कशुक्रतनुभवनपतिः स्यात्तदानीं मनुष्यः ॥<-

Also
->शुक्रेन्दुभ्यां संयुते नेत्रनाथे निश्यन्धः स्यात्पापदृष्टे शुभैर्न ।<-
->शुक्रार्कौ वा लग्नपेनैव युक्तौ पाताले वा रन्ध्रगे मध्यदृक् स्यात् ॥<-

->विलग्नवित्तास्ततपःसुतेशा रिपुव्ययच्छिद्रगृहोपयाताः ।<-
->विलग्नसंबन्धकरः सितश्चेद्दिलोचनाभावमुपैति जातः ॥ ६६ ॥<-

*Sloka 66.* If the lords of the 1st, 2nd, 7th, 9th and the 5th bhavas occupy the 6th, 12th and the 8th, and if Venus be associated with the Lagna, the person born will be blind.

->**NOTES.**<-

Some books read **सुखेशाः**
This sloka appears in **जातक तत्व** with a different reading for the 3rd पाद *viz.*, **विलग्नकर्मान्त्यगसंस्थिता वा**

<vocab>Jataka Parijata, Adhyaya, Sloka, Bhava, Lagna, Venus, Moon, Sun, night-blind, blindness, Jatakalankara, Jataka Tattva</vocab>

<page-num>785</page-num>
<header>Sl. 67-69 एकादशोऽध्यायः 785</header>

**सितः सुतारीशयुतो विलग्ने नरेशकोपान्नयनप्रमादः ।**
**धनेशभौमौ यदि लग्नयातौ कर्णस्य रोगं कथयन्ति तज्ज्ञाः ६७**

*Sloka 67.* When <term>Venus</term> occupies the <term>Lagna</term> in conjunction with the lord of the 5th and the 6th <term>bhavas</term>, the person born will run the risk of losing his eyes in consequence of the displeasure of his sovereign. When the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term> and <term>Mars</term> are in the <term>Lagna</term>, professors of astrology declare the effect to be ear-ache.

<margin>cf. जातकत्न</margin>
**राजारिनाथौ सहितौ विलग्ने कुजेन दृष्टे हि विलग्ननाथे ।**
**तथैव भौमेन निरीक्षितौ चेन्नृपालकोपान्नयनस्य नाशः ॥**
**शन्यारयोगे गुलिकेन युक्ते नेत्रेश्वरे तत्र तु नेत्ररोगः ।**
**नेत्रे यदा पापबहुत्वयोगे यमेन दृष्टे सति रुग्णनेत्रः ॥ ६८ ॥**

*Sloka 68.* When <term>Saturn</term> and <term>Mars</term> are together in the 2nd <term>bhava</term> which is occupied by its lord in conjunction with <term>माण्डि</term> (<term>Mandi</term>), the effect will be eye-disease. If there be several malefic planets in the 2nd <term>bhava</term> and <term>Saturn</term> aspect the same, the person born will have diseased eyes (will be blear-eyed).

->NOTES.<-

This sloka appears both in <term>जातकत्न</term> and <term>सर्वार्थचिन्तामणि</term>. A reference to these two works enables us to get at the antecedent of तत्र in the above sloka as referring to the 2nd <term>bhava</term>.

**नेत्रेश्वरे शुभयुते शुभदृष्टियुतेऽथवा ।**
**शुभांशकस्थिते चाऽपि शुभदृक् स नरो भवेत् ॥ ६९ ॥**

*Sloka 69.* When the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term> is associated with or aspected by <term>benefic planets</term> or occupies a <term>Navamsa</term> owned by them, the person born will have fine eyes.

<vocab>Venus, Lagna, bhava, Mars, Saturn, Mandi, eye-disease, ear-ache, Navamsa, benefic planets, जातकत्न, सर्वार्थचिन्तामणि</vocab>

--- Page 115 ---
<lang>sa, en</lang>
<page-num>735</page-num>
<header>जातकपारिजाते Adh. XI.</header>

<margin>cf. जातकरत्न</margin>
लग्नाधिपे सौम्यखगेन युक्ते बलान्विते कारकखेचरेन्द्रे ।
नेत्रे शुभे तद्भवनेश्वरो वा सौम्यान्वितः सौम्यदृशा समेतः ॥
जातोऽसौ सुमुखः शुभे धनगते तुङ्गादिवर्गान्विते
तद्भावे यदि सौम्यवर्गसहिते वाक्सिद्धिमेति ध्रुवम् ।
आज्यस्पर्शमुपैति वित्तगृहगे भौमे दिनेशेक्षिते
जातः कोद्रवमुख्यभुक् धनगते राहौ च पापेक्षिते ॥ ७० ॥

*Sloka* 70. When a benefic planet is in the 2nd bhava occupying its exaltation and other benefic vargas, the person born will be fair-faced. If the 2nd bhava consist of the vargas of an auspicious planet, he will without doubt acquire the faculty of speaking well. If Mars aspected by the Sun occupy the **धनभाव** (Dhana bhava), the person gets **आज्यस्पर्श** (Ajyasparsa). If Rahu aspected by a malefic planet occupy the 2nd bhava, he will have to subsist chiefly on the coarse grain called **कोद्रव** (Kodrava).

->NOTES.<-

This sloka appears to have been patched up out of two slokas from <term>जातकत्न</term>. The following is the latter half for the first two lines.

वागीशे बलसंयुते शुभयुते स्वोच्चादिवर्गस्थिते
देवेड्ये बलसंयुते शुभयुते वाक्सिद्धिमेति ध्रुवम् ॥

<margin>cf. सर्वार्थचिन्तामणि</margin>
मन्दात्मजेन सहिते यदि भूमिपुत्रे वित्तेश्वरा निधनराशिमुपागते वा ।
तेनैव वीक्षितयुते धनपाथनाथे त्वान्यस्पृशादि नियतं प्रादन्ति तज्ज्ञाः ॥

<term>आज्यस्पर्श</term> is said to be a kind of <term>Prayaschitta</term>.

लग्नास्तार्थाष्टमस्थेऽर्के भौमे वान्यतरेक्षिते ।
आज्यस्पर्शोऽग्निमीतिर्वा संभवन्ति महर्षिभिः ॥ ७१ ॥

<vocab>जातकपारिजाते, Adh. XI, Sloka 70, Sloka 71, bhava, vargas, Dhana bhava, Ajyasparsa, Rahu, Kodrava, Prayaschitta, जातकरत्न, सर्वार्थचिन्तामणि</vocab>

--- Page 116 ---
<lang>English, Sanskrit</lang>
<page-num>737</page-num>
<header>Sl 71-74 एकादशोऽध्यायः 737</header>

***Sloka 71.*** When one of the two planets—the Sun or Mars,—occupy the Lagna, the 7th, the 2nd or the 8th and is aspected by the other, <term>आज्यस्पर्श</term> (<term>Ajyasparsa</term>) or risk of fire or small-pox may happen.

---

->NOTES.<-

Compare the following two lines from <term>जातकभरण</term> which constitute the latter half of the last two lines of the previous sloka,

->लग्नात्कामधनाष्टराशिषु कुजे सूर्येऽथवा तद्वदेत्<-
->आज्यस्पर्शमथाग्निभीतिरथवा स्फोटादिभिः पीडनम् ॥<-

Also

->विलग्नासप्ताष्टमवित्तराशौ दिनेशयुक्ते क्षितिसूनुयुक्ते ।<-
->भौमेन वा वासरनाथदृष्टे स्फोटाग्निभीतिं प्रवदेदूव्रणाद्वा ॥<-

->पापैर्युते मुखस्थाने दुर्मुख: पापवीक्षिते ।<-
->क्रोधाननो नर: पापी तदीशे गुलिकान्विते ॥ ७२ ॥<-

***Sloka 72.*** When the 2nd bhava is occupied by malefic planets, the person born will be ugly-faced or foul-mouthed. If the bhava be aspected by a malefic planet, he will be fretful; if the lord of that bhava be associated with Gulika, he will be wicked.

->प्रफुल्लवदन: श्रीमान् केन्द्रे मुखपतौ यदा ।<-
->स्वोच्चस्वमित्रवर्गस्थे सुमुख: शुभवीक्षिते ॥ ७३ ॥<-

***Sloka 73.*** When the lord of the 2nd bhava occupies a Kendra, the person born will have a beaming face and be fortunate. If the planet be in its exaltation, in its own or a friendly varga and aspected by a benefiic planet, the person will be fair-faced.

->वाग्भावेशे राहुयुक्ते च दु:स्थे राहुक्रान्तस्थाननाथान्विते च ।<-
->पाके भुक्तौ तस्य दन्तामय: स्याद् जिह्वारोगं वारकाद्युभुशुकौ ७४<-

---

<sig>93</sig>

<vocab>Sloka, Lagna, Ajyasparsa, Jatakabharana, Bhava, Gulika, Kendra, Varga, Sanskrit, Jyotisha, Astrology, benefiic</vocab>

<header>जातकपारिजाते Adh. XI.</header>

---

*Sloka 74.* When the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term> associated with Rahu occupies a <term>दुःस्थान</term> (Dusthhana) and is in conjunction with the lord of the <term>Rasi</term> occupied by Rahu, the person born will have tooth disease in the ripening of the <term>dasa</term> of the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term> and in the sub-period belonging to that planet, and a tongue malady in the sub-period of Mercury.

<margin>cf. जातकज्ञ</margin>
->वाग्भावपे षष्ठगते सराहौ राहुस्थितार्क्षाधिपसंयुते वा ।<-
->दन्तादिरोगः पतने च तेषां भुक्तौ तयोर्वा प्रवदन्ति तज्ज्ञाः ॥<-

->राहुद्वितीयगृहपौ सहजेशयुक्तौ<-
->जातः समेति गलरोगमतीव कष्टम् ।<-
->दारिद्र्यदौ रविशनी धनराशिययातौ<-
->भौमार्कजौ सकलरोगकरौ भवेताम् ॥ ७५ ॥<-

*Sloka 75.* If Rahu and the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term> be in conjunction with the lord of the 3rd <term>bhava</term>, the person born will become liable to a throat disease of a severe type. The Sun and Saturn in the 2nd <term>bhava</term> engender poverty. Mars and Saturn in the same <term>bhava</term> produce every kind of disease.

->वाग्भावेशे गुरुयुते नाशस्थे मूकता भवेत् ।<-
->दोषकृन्न तु सर्वत्र सोच्चस्वर्क्षगतो ग्रहः ॥ ७६ ॥<-

*Sloka 76.* When the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term> is in the 8th place therefrom in conjunction with Jupiter, dumbness will be the consequence. In every case in which a planet is in exaltation or in its own house, it does not produce the evil referred to above.

---

->NOTES.<-

Compare with this the first half of sloka 79 *infra*.

<vocab>Rahu, bhava, Dusthhana, Rasi, dasa, Mercury, Sun, Saturn, Mars, Jupiter, Jataka Parijata</vocab>

<lang>Sanskrit, English</lang>

<header>Sl. 77-80 एकादशोऽध्यायः</header>

वागीशबुधजीवेषु निर्विद्यो नाशगेषु च ।
केन्द्रेषु ते त्रिकोणे वा स्वक्षे वा विद्ययान्वितः ॥ ७७ ॥

*Sloka 77.* If the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term>, Mercury and Jupiter occupy the 8th <term>bhava</term> from it, the person born will be destitute of learning. If they be in a <term>Kendra</term>, <term>Trikona</term> or in a <term>Swakshetra</term>, he will have acquaintance with some branch of knowledge.

द्वितीये दुर्बले सौम्ये द्वन्द्वयुद्धहतो भवेत् ।
जीवार्थेशौ दुर्बलौ वा पवनव्याधिमान्नरः ॥ ७८ ॥

*Sloka 78.* If Mercury be weak in the 2nd <term>bhava</term>, the person born will be killed in a duel. If Jupiter and the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term> be weak in the 2nd <term>bhava</term>, the person concerned will have wind disease.

वाक्स्थानपे देवपुरोहितेन युक्ते यदा नाशगते तु मूकः ।
वाक्स्थानपे सौम्ययुते त्रिकोणे केन्द्रस्थिते वा शुभदे च वाग्मी ॥

*Sloka 79.* When the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term> occupies the 8th place from it in conjunction with Jupiter, the person born will be a mute. A person would be eloquent when the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term>, occupies a <term>Trikona</term> or <term>Kendra</term> from it in conjunction with a benefic planet and is also itself benefic.

वागीशस्वांशपे सौम्ये स्वोच्चे वा शुभवीक्षिते ।
पारावतांशके वाऽपि वाग्मी पट्टतरो भवेत् ॥ ८० ॥

*Sloka 80.* When the planet owning the <term>Navamsa</term> occupied by the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term> is benefic and is in its exaltation or aspected by a benefic planet or is in a <term>पारावतांश</term> (<term>Paravathamsa</term>), the person born will be eloquent and clever.

<vocab>bhava, Kendra, Trikona, Swakshetra, wind disease, mute, eloquent, Navamsa, Paravathamsa, Mercury, Jupiter</vocab>

--- Page 119 ---
<lang>sa, en</lang>
<page-num>740</page-num>
<header>जातकपारिजाते Adh: XI</header>

---

केन्द्रत्रिकोणगे जीवे शुक्रे स्वोच्चं गते यदि ।
वाग्भावपेन्दुपुत्रे वा गणितज्ञो भवेन्नरः ॥ ८१ ॥

*Sloka 81.* A person will become a mathematician when Jupiter is in a <term>Kendra</term> or <term>Trikona</term>, and when Mercury owning the 2nd <term>bhava</term> or Venus is in exaltation.

गणितज्ञो भवेज्जातो वाग्भावे भूमिनन्दने ।
ससौम्ये बुधसंदृष्टे केन्द्रे वा सोमनन्दने ॥ ८२ ॥

*Sloka 82.* A person becomes a mathematician when Mars is in the 2nd <term>bhava</term> in conjunction with a benefic planet and Mercury aspects the same or occupies a <term>Kendra</term>.

वाग्भावपे रवौ भौमे गुरुशुक्रनिरीक्षिते ।
पारावतांशके वापि तर्कयुक्तिपरायणः ॥ ८३ ॥

*Sloka 83.* When the Sun or Mars being the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term> is aspected by Jupiter and Venus or has attained the पारावतांश (<term>Paravathamsa</term>), the person born will be devoted to the science of argumentation.

संपूर्णबलसंयुक्ते गुरौ तद्भुवनेश्वरे ।
दिनेशभृगुसंदृष्टे शाब्दिक़ोयं भवेन्नरः ॥ ८४ ॥

*Sloka 84.* When Jupiter in full strength is the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term> and is aspected by the Sun and Venus, the person born will become a grammarian.

<margin>cf. जातकलेख</margin>
गुरौ धनस्थे बलपूर्णयुक्ते शुक्रेण सूर्येण च दृष्टियुक्ते ।
शुक्रे धने स्वोच्चगतेऽयवापि जातो नरः शब्दविश़ेषशास्त्रवित् ॥

वेदान्तपरिशीलः स्यात् केन्द्रकोणे गुरौ यदि ।
बुधेन भृगुणा दृष्टे शनौ पारावतांशके ॥ ८५ ॥

<vocab>Jataka Parijata, Sloka, Kendra, Trikona, Bhava, Paravathamsa, mathematician, grammarian, argumentation, Jataka-lekha</vocab>

*Sloka 85.* A person will be devoted to Ontology when Jupiter occupying a Kendra or Trikona is aspected by Mercury and Venus, and Saturn is in a पारावतांश (Paravathamsa).

<margin>जातकरण</margin>
->धने बुधे स्वोच्चगते शनिस्तु पारावतस्थे भृगुरूत्तमांशे ।<-
->गुरौ भृगौ वा तनुभावयुक्ते वेदान्तविज्ञानपरायणः स्यात् ॥<-

->षट्शास्त्रवल्लभः केन्द्रे जीवे दानवपूजिते ।<-
->सिंहासने गोपुरांशे वाग्भावस्वांशपे बुधे ॥ ८६ ॥<-

*Sloka 86.* A person becomes a proficient in the six sciences when Jupiter is in a Kendra, Venus has attained a सिंहासनांश (Simhasanamsa) and Mercury, owning the Navamsa occupied by the planet in the 2nd bhava, is in a गोपुरांश (Gopuramsa).

<margin>cf. जातकरण</margin>
->षट्शास्त्रवित्केन्द्रगतेऽथ जीवे शुक्रे च वाग्भावगते बुधे च ॥<-
->उपजीव्य नरं सर्वे तदीशे गोपुरांशके ।<-
->वर्द्धन्ते मुदितास्तस्य भृगौ पारावतांशके ॥ ८७ ॥<-

*Sloka 87.* When the lord of the 2nd bhava has attained a Gopuramsa and Venus a Paravatamsa, the person born will have all his people living under his protection and prospering happily.

->नास्ति चेद्राजयोगं तु पुरस्कृत्य नरं जनाः ॥ ८८ ॥<-

*Sloka 88.* The latter half of this sloka is missing in all the manuscripts available and the sloka cannot therefore be made out.

->जापाकुडुम्बगृहपौ सितपापयुक्तौ<-
->दुःस्थौ च तत्समकलत्रहरौ भवेताम् ।<-
->विद्याधिपे बलवति स्वरराशिपे वा<-
->तुङ्गादिगे यदि समेति फलत्रमेकम् ॥ ८९ ॥<-

<vocab>Sloka, एकादशोऽध्यायः, Ontology, Jupiter, Kendra, Trikona, Mercury, Venus, Saturn, पारावतांश, Paravathamsa, जातकरण, वेदान्तविज्ञान, षट्शास्त्र, सिंहासनांश, Simhasanamsa, Navamsa, bhava, गोपुरांश, Gopuramsa, राजयोग</vocab>

<header>जातकपारिजाते Adh. XI.</header>

*Sloka 89.* The lords of the 7th and the 2nd <term>bhavas</term> if associated with Venus or malefic planets and badly placed, will cause the loss of one or more wives according as one or more planets are in conjunction with them in the <term>दुःस्थान</term> (<gloss>Dusthhana</gloss>). But if the lord of the 2nd or the 7th <term>bhava</term> be strong occupying its exaltation and other benefic <term>Vargas</term>, only one wife will fall to the lot of the person concerned.

भुक्तिस्थानपती सितेन्दुसहिते लग्नादिकेन्द्रस्थिते
रौप्यं पात्रमुपैति काञ्चनमयं जीवेन्दुशुक्रान्विते ।
भुक्तिस्थानपदेहपौ शनियुतौ लोहादिपात्रं वदेद्
भौमे पापनिरीक्षिते धनगते जातः कदन्नादिभुक् ॥ ९० ॥

*Sloka 90.* If the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term> occupy the <term>Lagna</term> or other <term>Kendra</term> in conjunction with Venus and the Moon, the person born will have a silver plate to eat out of. If the lord of the <term>भुक्तिस्थान</term> (<gloss>Bhukthisthana</gloss>) in the above circumstances be in conjunction with Jupiter, the Moon and Venus, the eating plate will be of gold. If the lords of the <term>Lagna</term> and the 2nd <term>bhava</term> be in conjunction with Saturn, the astrologer may declare the plate to be of iron or other less costly metal. If Mars aspected by an evil planet occupy the 2nd <term>bhava</term> the person born will have to subsist on bad or unwholesome food.

---

->NOTES.<-

This as well as the next sloka are found in <term>जातक-र</term><unclear>त्ने</unclear>.

यद्वायनो भुक्तिपतौ सपापे दावाग्निदण्डायुधकालभागे ।
नीचांशके पापनिरीक्षिते च शुभैर्न दोषः सहितेक्षिते वा ॥९१॥

*Sloka 91.* When the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term> associated with a malefic planet is in the 60th portions

<vocab>Jataka Parijata, bhava, Kendra, Lagna, Bhukthisthana, Dusthhana, Varga, Sloka, malefic, benefic</vocab>

<lang>Sanskrit, English</lang>
<page-num>743</page-num>
<header>Sl. 92-95 एकादशोऽध्यायः 743</header>

(Shashtyamsa) termed दावाग्नि (Davagni) दण्डायुध (Dandayuda) or काल (Kala) and in a depression Navamsa and aspected also by a malefic planet, the person born will be a glutton. If the planet owning the 2nd bhava be conjoined with or aspected by benefic planets, the evil mentioned above will not follow.

->भुक्तिस्थाने शुभयुते तदीशे शुभसंयुते ।<-
->शुभग्रहेण संदृष्टे सुखभुक् स नरो भवेत् ॥ ९२ ॥<-

*Sloka 92.* If a benefic planet occupy the 2nd bhava and the lord thereof be in conjunction with a benefic planet and if the bhava or the lord of the same or both be aspected by a benefic planet, the person born will eat agreeable meals.

->परान्नभुक् तदीशेऽपि नीचशत्रुसमन्विते ।<-
->नीचखेचरसंदृष्टे तद्दूषणपरायणः ॥ ९३ ॥<-

*Sloka 93.* When the lord of the 2nd bhava is in depression or in conjunction with an inimical planet and aspected by a planet in depression, the person born will eat meals supplied by another, reviling them at the same time.

->कालोचिताशनी भुक्तिनाथे लग्नेशवीक्षिते ।<-
->पापग्रहेण संदृष्टे नीचांशादियुते न तु ॥ ९४ ॥<-

*Sloka 94.* If the lord of the भुक्तिभाव (Bhuktibhava) be aspected by the lord of the Lagna, the person born will eat timely meals, but this will not be the case if the भुक्तिनाथ (Bhuktinatha) be in a depression Navamsa and aspected by a malefic planet.

->स्वल्पाशी रुचिकामः स्याद् भुक्तिनाथे शुभे गृहे ।<-
->स्वोच्चे शुभेन संदृष्टे मृदंशादिसमन्विते ॥ ९५ ॥<-

<vocab>Shashtyamsa, Davagni, Dandayuda, Kala, Navamsa, malefic, bhava, Sloka, Lagna, Bhuktibhava, Bhuktinatha, depression, benefic</vocab>

# जातकपारिजाते द्वादशोऽध्याय:
## ॥ तृतीयचतुर्थभावफलानि ॥
### Adhyaya XII.
### THE EFFECTS OF THE 3RD AND THE 4TH BHAVAS,
### ॥ अथ तृतीयभावफलम् ॥

ज्येष्ठानुजस्थिति पराक्रमसाहसानि
कण्ठस्वरश्रुतिवराभरणांशुकानि ।
धैर्यं च वीर्यबलमूलफलाशनानि
चक्ष्ये तृतीयभवनात् क्रमशोज्विलानि ॥ १ ॥

Sloka 1. I am going to treat of the following which are deducible from the 3rd bhava in the order of their enumeration: The welfare of the elder and younger brothers, enterprise, daring, voice, tone, ear, select ornaments, articles of apparel, steadiness, valour, strength, edible roots and fruits.

---

<margin>मानसाभरण</margin>
सहोदराणामथ किंकराणां पराक्रमाणांमुपजीविनां च ।
विचारणा जातकशास्त्रविद्भिस्तृतीयभावे नियमेन वाच्या ॥

<margin>जातकरत्न</margin>
तृतीयराशो: सहजाभिवृद्धि भक्ष्यं गुलं चापि पुनश्च कर्णम् ।
सहोदराणां क्रमशस्तु सख्यं मुक्तौ विशेषदपि मूलकादीन् ॥
भ्रातृस्थानं तृतीयं च नवैकादशसप्तमम् ।
तत्तदीशदशायां च भ्रातृलाभो भवेन्नृणाम् ॥ २ ॥

<vocab>जातकपारिजात (Jātakapārijāta), द्वादशोऽध्याय: (12th Chapter), तृतीयभाव (3rd House), चतुर्थभाव (4th House), फलानि (Effects), ज्येष्ठ (Elder brother), अनुज (Younger brother), पराक्रम (Valour), साहस (Daring), कण्ठस्वर (Voice), श्रुति (Ear/Hearing), आभरण (Ornaments), अंशुक (Apparel), धैर्य (Steadiness), वीर्य (Valour/Heroism), बल (Strength), मूल (Root), फल (Fruit), मानसाभरण (Mānasābharaṇa), सहोदर (Brother), किंकर (Servant), विचारणा (Deliberation), जातकरत्न (Jātaka Ratna), सहजाभिवृद्धि (Increase of siblings), भ्रातृस्थान (House of brothers)</vocab>

--- Page 124 ---
<lang>English, Sanskrit</lang>
<page-num>744</page-num>
<header>जातकपारिजाते Adh. XI.</header>

*Sloka 95.* A person will eat sparingly and be fond of palatable food when the <term>भुक्तिनाथ</term> (<term>Bhukthinatha</term>) is in an auspicious house in exaltation, aspected by a benefic planet and occupies a benefic <term>षष्ठ्यंश</term> (<term>Shashtyamsa</term>) such as is termed <term>मृदु</term> (<term>Mridu</term>)

->भुक्तीश्वरे केन्द्रगतं बलाढ्ये स्वोच्चस्थिते वा ग्रहवीक्षिते वा ।<-
->शुभग्रहाः केन्द्रगताश्च सर्वे जातस्तु भुङ्क्तेऽल्पमतीव सूक्ष्मम् ॥<-

->भुक्तिस्थानाधिपे मन्दे तदीशे वाऽर्कसंयुते ।<-
->नीचेर्कसूनुना दृष्टे श्राद्धभुक् सततं नरः ॥ ९६ ॥<-

*Sloka 96.* When the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term> is Saturn or in conjunction with Saturn, or is aspected in depression by Saturn, the person born will be continuously eating in <term>Sraddhas</term>.

->सिंहासनांशे यदि देवपूज्ये शुक्रे यदा गोपुराभागयुक्ते ।<-
->ऐरावतांशे धनपे बलाढ्ये जातस्त्वसंख्याश्रितरक्षकः स्यात् ९७<-

->॥ इति जातकपारिजाते एकादशोऽध्यायः ॥<-

*Sloka 97.* When Jupiter is in a <term>सिंहासनांश</term> (<term>Simhasanamsa</term>), Venus in a <term>गोपुरांश</term> (<term>Gopuramsa</term>) and the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term> being possessed of strength occupies an <term>ऐरावतांश</term> (<term>Iravathamsa</term>), the person born will maintain an untold number of dependents.

### **NOTES.**

This sloka is also in <term>जातकच</term>.

->**End of the 11th Adhyaya.**<-
