<header>Sl. 6-7 द्वादशोऽध्यायः 747</header>

->सोदरेशकुजौ नाशं गतौ चेत्सोदरक्षयः ।<-
->पापर्क्षगौ सपापौ वा भ्रातृनुत्पाद्य नाशयौ ॥ ६ ॥<-

*Sloka 6.* If the lord of the 3rd bhava and Mars occupy the 8th, there will be loss of brothers. If the two planets be in a malefic sign or in conjunction with a malefic planet, they will bring brothers into existence and take them away.

<margin>जातकर्म</margin>
->नाशस्थितौ सोदरनाथभौमौ पापेक्षितौ सोदरनाशमाहुः ।<-
->पापर्क्षगौ पापसमागमौ वा भ्रातॄन्ममुत्पाद्य विनाशहेतुः ॥<-
->नीचर्क्षगौ सोदरकारकेशौ नीचांशगौ पापसमागमौ वा ।<-
->क्रूरादिषष्ट्यंशगतौ तदानीं भ्रातॄन् समुत्पाद्य विनाशहेतुः ॥ ७ ॥<-

*Sloka 7.* If the lord of the 3rd bhava and its <term>कारक</term> (<term>Karaka</term> i.e. Mars ; *vide* Adhyaya II. *sl.* 51) occupy their depression signs or depression Navamsas, or be in conjunction with malefic planets or in malefic 60th portions <term>षष्ट्यंश</term> (<term>Shashtyamsa</term>) of a sign such as <term>क्रूर</term> (<term>Krura</term>) and others, their influence will be to bring brothers into the world and to remove them thence prematurely.

->NOTES.<-

The first <term>पाद</term> reads thus in <term>जातकर्म</term>.
. . नीचास्तगौ सोदरकारकाख्यौ.

<margin>उत्तरपाराशर</margin>
->तृतीयपतिमङ्गलौ स्वगृहतुङ्गगेहागौ<-
->मिथो भवति मित्रगौ शुभयुतौ शुभप्रेक्षितौ ।<-
->सुवीर्यद्युतिविक्रमं प्रमुखसद्गुणैर्मिश्रितः<-
->शुभेन दलमन्यथा फलति नीचपापान्विते ॥<-

<vocab>Jyotisha, bhava, Mars, Karaka, Navamsa, Shashtyamsa, Krura, Jatakarman, Uttara Parashara, astrology</vocab>

--- Page 126 ---
<lang>English, Sanskrit</lang>
<page-num>746</page-num>
<header>जातकपारिजाते Adh. XII.</header>

*Sloka* 2. The place where a brother can be look-
ed for is the 3rd, the 9th, the 11th or the 7th bhava
from the Lagna. In the dasa period of the lord of any
one of the several bhavas named, men may have an
acquisition in the form of a brother.

भ्रातृस्थानेशतद्राशितद्भावस्थद्युचारिणाम् ।
मध्ये बलसमेतस्य दशा सोदरवृद्धिदा ॥ ३ ॥

*Sloka* 3 Find which of these three is strongest :
*viz.* (1) the lord of the 3rd bhava ; (2) the lord owning
the Rasi occupied by the same ; (3) the planet occupying
the 3rd bhava. The dasa of the strongest of these
planets will lead to the increase of brothers.

भौमे बलविहीने चेद्दीर्घायुर्भ्रातृगे भवेत् ।
विलग्नगो बली यस्य कारकः स प्रभुः स्मृतः ॥ ४ ॥

*Sloka* 4. If Mars occupying the 3rd bhava be
without strength, the person will have long life. If the
<term>कारक</term> (Karaka) of the <term>भ्रातृभाव</term> (Bratrubhava) *i.e.* Mars be in
the Lagna, the person will be powerful.

जन्मकाले गुणी प्राणी कारको यः समृद्धिकृत् ।
क्षयकारी वियत्स्थो वा भावपोऽपि बलाधिकः ॥ ५ ॥

*Sloka* 5. The <term>कारक</term> (Karaka) of the 3rd bhava that
is strong and at an advantageous position at the time of
a person's birth, produces prosperity. The lord of the
3rd bhava even when endowed with strength leads to
decay when in the 10th place from the Lagna.

->NOTES.<-

It will be observed that the latter half of this sloka is merely
an illustration of the general principle enunciated in XI—4, 8 and
65 *supra*.

<vocab>Jātakapārijāta, Sloka, bhava, Lagna, dasa, Karaka, Rasi, Mars, Bratrubhava, Viyatstha</vocab>

*Sloka 2.* The place where a brother can be looked for is the 3rd, the 9th, the 11th or the 7th bhava from the Lagna. In the dasa period of the lord of any one of the several bhavas named, men may have an acquisition in the form of a brother.

*Sloka 3* Find which of these three is strongest : viz. (1) the lord of the 3rd bhava ; (2) the lord owning the Rasi occupied by the same ; (3) the planet occupying the 3rd bhava The dasa of the strongest of these planets will lead to the increase of brothers.

*Sloka 5.* The <term>कारक</term> (Karaka) of the 3rd bhava that is strong and at an advantageous position at the time of a person's birth, produces prosperity. The lord of the 3rd bhava even when endowed with strength leads to decay when in the 10th place from the Lagna.

<header>Sl. 11-13. द्वादशोऽध्यायः</header>
<meta>Printed text in English and Devanagari script. Some minor ink speckling throughout.</meta>

born. Saturn in such a position removes the next younger brother; and Mars, if in the same position, does away with all the younger brothers.

->NOTES.<-

This and the next nine slokas are also found in जातकतत्त्व.

> त्रिकोणकेन्द्रे यदि पापखेटे तृतीयभावादनुजस्य नाशम् ।
> शुभोपयाते सहजाभिवृद्धिः शुभाशुभं मिश्रफलं वदन्ति ॥ ११ ॥

Sloka 11. If a malefic planet occupy a Trikona or a Kendra from the 3rd bhava, the consequence will be the loss of the next younger brother. If a benefic planet occupy such a position, it will redound to the prosperity of the younger brother. If there be a combination of good and bad planets in the positions referred to, the effect will be mixed.

> दुःस्थे चन्द्रे सोदरखामियुक्ते जातस्यान्यस्तन्यपानं वदन्ति ।
> मातृभ्रातृस्थानपौ बन्धुयातौ नास्ति भ्रातृस्थानवृद्धिर्नराणाम् ॥ १२ ॥

*Sloka 12.* If the Moon in conjunction with the lord of the 3rd bhava occupy a दुःस्थान (Dusthhana), the person born, say the astrologers, will suck the breast of one, not his own mother. When the lords of the 3rd and the 4th bhavas are in the latter, the भ्रातृभाव (Bhratru-bhava flourishes not.

> भौमान्वितौ सोदरदौ भवेतामन्यैः समेतौ यदि नानुजः स्यात् ।
> सौरस्तृतीयेऽनुजनाशकर्ता विधुंतुदः सोदरवृद्धिकृत्स्यात् ॥ १३ ॥

*Sloka 13.* If the lords of the 3rd and the 4th bhavas be in conjunction with Mars, they lead to the birth of an younger brother; if in conjunction with other planets, there may be no younger brother. Saturn in the 3rd bhava brings about the loss of the next born;

<vocab>Sloka, Trikona, Kendra, bhava, Dusthhana, Bhratru-bhava, जातकतत्त्व, Saturn, Mars, Moon</vocab>

->भ्रातुः कारकैौ यदातृनिजदित्रान्योोन्यतुङ्गागतौ<-
->दुःस्थानं परिहृत्यतस्तु भवतः प्रख्यातसौभ्रातृकः ।<-
->दुःस्थानेन भवेच्छुभग्रहयुतौ तत्रैव तानल्पशो<-
->नीचाद्यस्तमयगतौ नहितद्वा स्त्राभीष्टिनश्चेद्बहुः ॥<-

->अतिक्रूरसमायुक्ते भावे वा कारकेऽपि वा ।<-
->तद्भावेनायके वाऽपि बाल्ये सोदरनाशनम् ॥ ८ ॥<-

> *Sloka 8.* When the 3rd <term>bhava</term>, its <term>कारक</term> (Karaka) or its lord is associated with a very malefic planet, the person born will lose brothers in his early years.

->धनेश्वरे नाशगते बलाढ्ये पापान्विते सोदरकारकाख्ये ।<-
->तन्मातृकारग्रहसंयुते च सापत्नमातुः सहजा वदन्ति ॥ ९ ॥<-

> *Sloka 9.* If the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term> be in great strength in the 8th <term>bhava</term> and if the <term>भ्रातृकारक</term> (Bhratru-karaka) be in conjunction with a malefic planet and also with the lord of the 4th place from the 3rd <term>bhava</term>, the person born, say the astrologers, will have brothers from a step mother.

->भ्रातृस्थाने यदि शुभयुते सोदराणां चिरायुः<-
->पापाक्रान्ते सहजभवने पापदृष्टे विनाशम् ।<-
->ज्येष्ठं हन्ति द्युमणिरनुजस्थानगः पापदृष्टः<-
->सौरस्तस्यानुजमवनिजो हन्ति सर्वान् कनिष्ठान् ॥ १० ॥<-

> *Sloka 10.* If the 3rd <term>bhava</term> be occupied by benefic planets, the brothers of the person born (say the astrologers) will have long life. If there be malefic planets in the <term>bhava</term> and malefic ones aspect it also, the brothers will die prematurely. The Sun occupying the 3rd <term>bhava</term> and aspected by a malefic planet kills the elder

<header>Sl. 11-13. द्वादशोऽध्यायः 749</header>
<warning>Speckled image with some printing artifacts; certain characters in both English and Sanskrit are slightly distorted.</warning>

born. Saturn in such a position removes the next younger brother; and Mars, if in the same position, does away with all the younger brothers.

->NOTES.<-

This and the next nine slokas are also found in जातकल्न.

त्रिकोणकेन्द्रे यदि पापखेते तृतीयभावादनुजस्य नाशम् ।
शुभोपयाते सहजाभिवृद्धिः शुभाशुभं मिश्रफलं वदन्ति ॥ ११ ॥

*Sloka 11.* If a malefic planet occupy a <term>Trikona</term> or a <term>Kendra</term> from the 3rd <term>bhava</term>, the consequence will be the loss of the next younger brother. If a benefic planet occupy such a position, it will redound to the prosperity of the younger brother. If there be a combination of good and bad planets in the positions referred to, the effect will be mixed.

दुःस्थे चन्द्रे सोदरखामियुक्ते जातस्यान्यस्तन्यपानं वदन्ति ।
मातृभ्रातृस्थानपौ बन्धुयातौ नास्ति भ्रातृस्थानवृद्धिर्नराणाम् ॥१२॥

*Sloka 12.* If the Moon in conjunction with the lord of the 3rd <term>bhava</term> occupy a <term>दुःस्थान</term> (**Dusthana**), the person born, say the astrologers, will suck the breast of one, not his own mother. When the lords of the 3rd and the 4th <term>bhavas</term> are in the latter, the <term>भ्रातृभाव</term> (**Bhratru-bhava** flourishes not.

भौमान्वितौ सोदरपौ भवेतामन्यैः समेतौ यदि नानुजः स्यात् ।
सौरस्तृतीयेऽनुजनाशकर्ता विधुन्तुदः सोदरवृद्धिकृत्स्यात् ॥ १३ ॥

*Sloka 13.* If the lords of the 3rd and the 4th <term>bhavas</term> be in conjunction with Mars, they lead to the birth of an younger brother; if in conjunction with other planets, there may be no younger brother. Saturn in the 3rd <term>bhava</term> brings about the loss of the next born ;

->भ्रातुः कारकपौ यदात्मनिजाद्वित्रान्योन्यतुङ्गगतौ<-
->दुःस्थानं परिहृत्यतस्तु भवतः प्रख्यातसौभ्रातृकः ।<-
->दुःस्थानेन भवेच्छुभग्रहयुतौ तत्रैव तानल्पशो<-
->नीचास्तमयगतौ नहितद्वा स्वाभीषितश्चेद्वहुः ॥<-

->अतिक्रूरसमायुक्ते भावे वा कारकेऽपि वा ।<-
->तद्भावेनायके वापि बाल्ये सोदरनाशनम् ॥ ८ ॥<-

*Sloka* 8. When the 3rd <term>bhava</term>, its <term>कारक</term> (<term>Karaka</term>) or its lord is associated with a very malefic planet, the person born will lose brothers in his early years.

->धनेश्वरे नाशगते बलाढ्ये पापान्विते सोदरकारकाख्ये ।<-
->तन्मातृकारकग्रहसंयुते च सापत्नमातुः सहजा वदन्ति ॥ ९ ॥<-

*Sloka* 9. If the lord of the 2nd <term>bhava</term> be in great strength in the 8th <term>bhava</term> and if the <term>भ्रातृकारक</term> (<term>Bhratru-karaka</term>) be in conjunction with a malefic planet and also with the lord of the 4th place from the 3rd <term>bhava</term>, the person born, say the astrologers, will have brothers from a step mother.

->भ्रातृस्थाने यदि शुभयुते सोदराणां चिरायुः<-
->पापाक्रान्ते सहजभवने पापदृष्टे विनाश्यम् ।<-
->ज्येष्ठं हन्ति द्युमणिरनुजस्थानगः पापदृष्टः<-
->सौरस्तस्यानुजमवनिजो हन्ति सर्वान् कनिष्ठान् ॥ १० ॥<-

*Sloka* 10. If the 3rd <term>bhava</term> be occupied by benefic planets, the brothers of the person born (say the astrologers) will have long life. If there be malefic planets in the <term>bhava</term> and malefic ones aspect it also, the brothers will die prematurely. The Sun occupying the 3rd <term>bhava</term> and aspected by a malefic planet kills the elder

while Rahu in the same bhava adds to the prosperity of the brothers that follow.

->अदृश्यराशौ यदि वा सपापे वधुगृहस्थे सहजाधिनाथे ।<-
->जातानुजस्योपरि नानुजः स्याद् पुंवर्गे दृश्यगते तथैव ॥ १४ ॥<-

*Sloka 14.* If the lord of the 3rd bhava occupy an invisible sign or be in the 7th bhava in conjunction with a malefic planet, the person born will have one younger brother and no more. The same will be the case if the lord of the भ्रातृभाव (Bhratru-bhava) occupy a male वर्ग (Varga) in a visible sign and be also in conjunction with a malefic planet.

->भ्रातृस्थानपतौ तु पुंभवनगे तस्यानुजो जायते<-
->युग्मर्क्षे यदि भार्गवेण शशिना युक्तेऽथवा वीक्षिते ।<-
->सौम्यर्क्षे शुभखेचरेशितयुते केन्द्रत्रिकोणस्थिते<-
->पश्चाज्जातसहोदरश्चिरसुखी दीर्घायुरारोग्यभाक् ॥ १५ ॥<-

*Sloka 15.* If the lord of the 3rd bhava be in a male sign (*vide Adhyaya I, Sl. 13*), the owner of the horoscope will have a younger brother born after him. If the planet in question should be associated with or aspected by Venus and the Moon in an even sign, or if it occupy a *Kendra* or *Trikona* identical with an auspicious sign in conjunction with or aspected by a benefic planet, the after-born brother will be long-lived, healthy and continuously happy.

->सहोदरस्थानपतौ तनुस्थे सलग्नपे सोदरनायके वा ।<-
->गर्भोऽभयोऽन्तरमस्य जातस्तृतीयराशौ सप्तौ तथैव ॥ १६ ॥<-

*Sloka 16.* If the lord of the 3rd bhava be in the *Lagna* or in conjunction with its lord, the child born next to the native will be secure from danger. If the

<header>Sl. 17-18 द्वादशोऽध्यायः 751</header>
<meta>Image contains text in Sanskrit (Devanagari script) and English. The text describes astrological principles regarding siblings based on planetary positions.</meta>

3rd <term>bhava</term> be occupied by its lord, the effect is the same.

-> लाभावसानभवनोपगतग्रहेन्द्र- <-
-> संख्यास्तदग्रजजनाः सहजा भवन्ति । <-
-> लग्नात् तृतीयधनयातवियच्चरेन्द्र- <-
-> संख्याजनाः स्युरनुजाः कथयन्ति तज्ज्ञाः ॥ १७ ॥ <-

**Sloka 17.** A person may have as many elder born as there are planets in the 11th and the 12th <term>bhavas</term> from the <term>Lagna</term> taken together. The after-born will be as many as the number of planets in the 3rd and the 2nd <term>bhavas</term> from the <term>Lagna</term>.

-> भ्रातृस्थानपकारकेक्षितयुता वीर्याधिका यद्यदा <-
-> तद्युक्तग्रहसंख्ययाऽनुजजनं जातः समेति ध्रुवम् । <-
-> चत्वारो यदि नीचमूढरिपुगा निघ्नन्ति जातानुजान् <-
-> ते कुर्वन्ति चिरायुष्वमलिनः सर्वानुजानां ग्रहाः ॥ १८ ॥ <-

**Sloka 18.** The sisters and brothers that a person may have born after him will be as many, at all events, as there are planets in his horoscope conjoined with the lord of the <term>भ्रातृभाव</term> (<term>Bhratrubhava</term>,) its <term>कारक</term> (<term>Karaka</term>), the planet aspecting it and the one occupying it, provided out of these four an account is taken only of those that may predominate in strength. If the four planets already mentioned be depressed, eclipsed* or in an inimical sign, their influence will be to do away with every brother or sister as they are born. In case these planets be friendly and possessed of strength, they secure long life to every one of the person's younger brothers and sisters.

-> भ्रातृस्थानपमुख्यखेटचतुषु द्वौ वीर्यवन्तौ यदा <-
-> नाशानाशफलप्रदौ समतया वीर्याधिकास्त्रयः । <-

<vocab>bhava, Lagna, Sloka, Bhratrubhava, Karaka, horoscope, planets, astrology</vocab>
